cua-driver-rs-v0.35.2cua-driver-rs: v0.35.2 Prerelease2026-10-13T14:17:34Z

Cua Driver 0.35.2

Summary

This release includes 3 fixes.

Fixes

  • name the missing AT-SPI bus in the X11 background keyboard refusal. (#5070)
  • scroll an inner Electron scroller in the background on macOS. (#5079)
  • tolerate a transient UIA read and a twice-reported consent prompt on Windows. (#5082)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Patch Changes

@upstash/context7-mcp@4.4.0 Stable2026-10-13T13:35:24Z

Minor Changes

  • 2d57b23: Add a foreign mode to the OAuth audience check on the hosted HTTP transport. MCP_OAUTH_AUDIENCE_ENFORCEMENT=foreign answers HTTP 401 (WWW-Authenticate: Bearer error="invalid_token", "This token was issued for another server. If you connect through a gateway or proxy, use a Context7 API key instead.") to a Clerk OAuth JWT whose aud names another server, and still admits a token with no aud, which grants authorized before Clerk's "Include Audience" setting carry on every refresh. observe (the default) and required are unchanged. The query string of an audience on an allowed host is ignored, since Cursor writes the configured MCP URL (https://mcp.context7.com/mcp?client=codex) into resource; on any other host it is kept. The audienceMismatch log line now carries outcome=accepted|rejected and is written at most once per token per hour instead of every ten minutes.

Patch Changes

  • 2882596: Verify Microsoft Entra token signatures before looking up the audience, and cap the Entra config cache.
  • 615e72b: Update the Next.js examples in the tool descriptions, the CLI rule and the bundled skill: the version example is now /vercel/next.js/v16.2.9, and the prompts use Next.js 16.

View release →

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v2026.10.3openclaw 2026.10.3 Stable2026-10-13T13:49:25Z

2026.10.3

Highlights

  • Updates that were stuck behind lost leases, completed-but-unrecognized receipts, or legacy 2026.9.8 activation records now recover and finish instead of refusing every retry. (#168388, #169038, #169027, #170195, #170170) Thanks @k94118, @mkdelta221, @waynegault, @chedong, and @Warhangel.
  • Windows, macOS, and pnpm updates no longer leave the Gateway stopped or roll back: machine-qualified task accounts, short-name install paths, Doctor ancestry re-checks, protected Bun service commands, and pnpm 12 launchers all keep working. (#169481, #169292, #169563, #170006, #170003, #170131) Thanks @RomneyDa, @jokerman1302-eng, @xilopaint, and @k-sekula.
  • Doctor no longer destroys data during repair: unreadable legacy auth keeps OAuth profiles, malformed UTF-8 memory files stay byte-exact, and blocked plugins keep their enabled state. (#170256, #169029, #168111) Thanks @Yolo521-bot, @wangmiao0668000666, and @obviyus.
  • The "On this Mac" first run on a fresh Mac authenticates the app-hosted Gateway instead of failing with "Gateway rejected auth", and the Mac app reconnects after a trusted certificate renewal. (#169403, #168151)
  • Chat-requested updates and plugin upgrades no longer roll back during Doctor cleanup, and stale Codex runtimes no longer break turns after an update. (#170218, #170306, #170339) Thanks @RomneyDa, @hannesrudolph, and @ezimerman.
  • Agent turns are more reliable: the next turn after native compaction, ChatGPT WebSocket closes mid-stream, switching back to OpenAI without the Codex plugin, and Codex follow-ups after failed thread preparation all succeed. (#169583, #167512, #169412, #169431) Thanks @obviyus, @ericcurtin, @mightyx, @jjjhenriksen, and @RomneyDa.

Changes

  • Meta: Muse Wonder is available as a model. (#170232) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • MiniMax: M3.1 models honor the selected thinking effort level, and OAuth sign-ins refresh expired tokens at the issuing regional endpoint instead of failing about two hours after login. (#160089, #160459) Thanks @NianJiuZst, @php737, @masatohoshino, and @rongtianhua.

Fixes

  • Doctor: keep canonical OAuth profiles when the legacy auth store is unreadable or malformed instead of deleting them. (#170256) Thanks @Yolo521-bot.
  • Doctor: never rewrite root memory files with replacement characters, and reject malformed UTF-8 before path set writes an edited file. (#169029, #168491) Thanks @wangmiao0668000666 and @Takhoffman.
  • Doctor: preserve installed plugins when path-safety checks block them instead of stripping their enabled flag when run as root. (#168111) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Doctor: include Canvas and Memory Core files in recovery copies, keep captures stable while workers stay open, honor Windows permissions for SQLite recovery snapshots, and stop spurious backups for unchanged one-shot cron jobs. (#169436, #169516, #170022, #169397, #168938) Thanks @RomneyDa, @harshitgupta31415, and @WG-Mojo.
  • Updates: recover blocked updates after receipt identity drift, lost handoff leases, legacy package metadata drift, and FreeBSD package recovery refusals. (#168388, #169038, #169027, #168775) Thanks @k94118, @mkdelta221, @waynegault, and @chedong.
  • Updates: older updaters no longer refuse candidates over bookkeeping-only config changes, and npm recovery reclaims retained runtimes that blocked it. (#170195, #170170) Thanks @Warhangel.
  • Updates: keep the real failure when git-update cleanup reporting is unavailable, route migrated-no-rollback failures to openclaw update repair, and stop reporting successful handoffs as failures. (#169454, #168993) Thanks @sayitfast and @RomneyDa.
  • Updates: clear repaired migration warnings, tolerate clock jumps during verification, and name the database and reason in capture and config-read warnings. (#169004, #169282, #169284) Thanks @WG-Mojo and @DonnieFi.
  • Updates: preserve the service heap limit in update processes, stop copying unrelated parent dependencies, and keep npm-global updates from retaining unrelated global packages. (#168889, #168927, #169551) Thanks @bluet, @RomneyDa, and @holgergruenhagen.
  • Updates: ride out briefly locked state databases, keep update-history writes from stalling Gateway cron and cleanup, and prevent false ownership loss during upgrades. (#168210, #169283, #169915) Thanks @DonnieFi and @vincentkoc.
  • Updates: Doctor handles unreadable supervisors and child binding errors, clean service-install refusals stay out of native-authority recovery, and openclaw update repair no longer reports success when Doctor maintenance was skipped. (#169665, #169658, #167714)
  • Windows: recognize machine-qualified Scheduled Task accounts, keep updater workers on short-name or miscased install paths, and stop rollbacks from Doctor ancestry re-checks. (#169481, #169292, #169563) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • macOS: unblock updates with protected Bun service commands, authenticate the app-hosted first run, and renew learned TLS pins on automatic reconnects. (#170006, #169403, #168151) Thanks @jokerman1302-eng.
  • pnpm: keep Gateway service commands on the durable install link and keep pnpm 12 launchers working after native updates. (#170003, #170131) Thanks @xilopaint and @k-sekula.
  • Plugins: preserve webhook listeners during deferred plugin upgrades, unblock repairs while data migrations are pending, and stop stale Codex runtimes breaking turns after updates. (#169035, #170339, #170306) Thanks @sirius1024 and @RomneyDa.
  • Plugins: catalog refreshes no longer retain duplicate native plugin graphs and recover their config after a failed native ESM refresh. (#169272, #169321) Thanks @obviyus and @RomneyDa.
  • Gateway: Claude CLI session tools keep working after the turn that started the MCP loopback ends, and channel tools no longer fail with stale caller authority after a config write restarts the account. (#165432, #159396) Thanks @Oldrich333, @Leit-motif, @ceckert, and @RomneyDa.
  • Gateway: node requests and events no longer fail with "pairing changed" after unrelated pairing activity or while another device's pairing is written. (#169486, #169575)
  • Gateway: restart cleanly when a stored cloud worker's machine is gone, preserve live sends during outbound queue recovery, and resume steered sessions after restart. (#168907, #169010, #170252) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Agents: prevent next-turn failures after native compaction without loading entire large transcripts. (#169583, #108851) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Models: switching back to an OpenAI model works when the Codex plugin is disabled, and model selections without an activatable harness are rejected. (#169412, #134837) Thanks @obviyus and @goslingmanagment.
  • OpenAI: turns no longer fail when the ChatGPT WebSocket closes with code 1000 mid-stream. (#167512) Thanks @ericcurtin, @obviyus, and @mightyx.
  • Codex: recover follow-up requests after failed thread preparation. (#169431) Thanks @jjjhenriksen and @RomneyDa.
  • Networking: guarded model requests fall back to IPv6 when IPv4 is unreachable, keeping SSRF validation. (#168973) Thanks @an2in and @georgenaz.
  • Copilot: honor reported context capacity when native limits are missing. (#156636) Thanks @saju01 and @RomneyDa.
  • Anthropic: guide Claude CLI to the registered OpenClaw tools. (#153032) Thanks @kiranvk-2011.
  • DeepSeek: recover calls markup instead of leaking tool payloads into replies. (#169728) Thanks @TOMUIV.
  • Release: first-hop update coverage admits sources by schema version, failed receipts recover without rerunning workloads, and upgrade diagnostics keep failing steps. (#169012, #168266, #165267, #169567, #169084, #168191, #165968, #164789, #168097) Thanks @RomneyDa and @shakkernerd.

v2026.10.5-beta.3openclaw 2026.10.5-beta.3 Prerelease2026-10-13T10:00:37Z

2026.10.5-beta.3

Highlights

  • Updates leave the Gateway running: pnpm 12 installs keep their Gateway service and openclaw launcher on the durable install path, Windows updates accept qualified Scheduled Task accounts and their own verified driver, macOS accepts protected Bun service commands, and clean service-install refusals no longer leave the Gateway stopped. (#170003, #169059, #170131, #169244, #169481, #169563, #170006, #169662, #169658, #169915) Thanks @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @RomneyDa, @jokerman1302-eng, and @vincentkoc.
  • Stuck updates recover: updates settle package-activation journals after filesystem remounts or device-number drift, ride out a briefly locked state database, stop reporting false post-install failures after migration, and reclaim retained updater runtimes that blocked npm recovery. (#169410, #170046, #169050, #169352, #168210, #170130, #169422, #170170, #169463) Thanks @patrickschiller, @RomneyDa, @SergeiAug, @angeliti999, and @Warhangel.
  • Doctor keeps credentials and data: Doctor no longer deletes an OAuth profile when the legacy auth store is unreadable, refuses retired state before migrating the database, imports retained ACP metadata, and restores a stopped Gateway when cleanup fails. (#170256, #168959, #170051, #169918, #169759, #169783) Thanks @Yolo521-bot, @bautrey, and @RomneyDa.
  • Turns survive restarts: interrupted turns, subagent work, automation turns, steered sessions, and node-hosted turns resume after a Gateway restart, and streamed replies from a killed worker stay in history. (#168650, #169144, #170162, #170252, #170161, #170223)
  • Telegram never replays old updates: the first start after updating no longer re-dispatches months-old Telegram updates from the legacy spool as new owner instructions. (#170068, #169784) Thanks @angeliti999.
  • Codex and provider replies complete: Codex replies keep their last tokens and accept follow-ups after a failed thread preparation, ChatGPT turns retry a mid-stream WebSocket close, and DeepSeek tool markup no longer leaks into messages. (#168400, #169431, #167512, #167345, #169728, #159665) Thanks @RomneyDa, @jjjhenriksen, @ericcurtin, @obviyus, @mightyx, and @TOMUIV.
  • Meta Muse Wonder: Meta users can select meta/muse-wonder-1.0 with tools, image input, streaming, reasoning controls, and a 1M-token context window. (#170232) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Plugin SDK compatibility: plugins built against 2026.10.1 and 2026.10.2 can import createSessionHeaderLink from openclaw/plugin-sdk/control-ui again. (#169746) Thanks @RomneyDa.

Changes

  • Meta Muse Wonder: adds meta/muse-wonder-1.0 with text and image input, tools, streaming, encrypted reasoning replay, and a 1,048,576-token context window; Spark remains the onboarding default. (#170232) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Device joins: the CLI and Control UI join command now installs a background node service that can host agent sessions (--service --session-host), with a command-only service alternative, and already-paired devices get a working command to enable session hosting. (#168623) Thanks @obviyus.

Fixes

  • Telegram: legacy spool updates older than 24 hours are imported as stale and never dispatched, so already-handled messages are not executed again after an update. (#170068, #169784) Thanks @angeliti999.
  • Plugin SDK compatibility: createSessionHeaderLink is restored in openclaw/plugin-sdk/control-ui as a deprecated export for plugins built against 2026.10.1 and 2026.10.2. (#169746) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Reply delivery: streamed text from a killed native worker stays in history with its real failure, steered messages behind subagent announcements are no longer dropped as interrupted, blocking questions from routed agent turns reach their conversation, live sends survive delivery recovery, WebChat no longer shows false attachment errors or duplicate file cards, Talk consultations no longer abort right after connecting, and deleting a session records its cancellation before draining retained replies. (#170223, #168077, #170039, #169010, #169093, #170083) Thanks @RomneyDa and @brokemac79.
  • Restart recovery: turns interrupted while a session was still preparing resume after a restart, interrupted subagents return to their parent with their history, automation turns, steered sessions, and node-hosted turns resume instead of failing with an active turn claim, a Gateway restarts cleanly when a stored cloud worker's machine is gone, and symlinked state directories keep working after a restart. (#168650, #169144, #170162, #170252, #170161, #168907, #168361) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Nodes and channels: node requests and events no longer fail with "pairing changed" during another device's pairing activity, and channel tools keep working after a config write restarts the account. (#169486, #169575, #159396) Thanks @ceckert and @RomneyDa.
  • macOS app: fresh app installs authenticate to their app-hosted Gateway and finish setup faster, and the Gateway reconnects automatically after a trusted TLS certificate renewal. (#169403, #168151)
  • Codex and OpenAI: Codex replies keep their final tokens when a stream drops the last delta, Codex follow-ups work after a failed thread preparation, ChatGPT turns retry a mid-stream WebSocket close with code 1000, and personal-account quota rechecks no longer abort replies. (#168400, #169431, #167512, #167345, #170065) Thanks @RomneyDa, @jjjhenriksen, @ericcurtin, @obviyus, and @mightyx.
  • Models and providers: DeepSeek tool-call markup is recovered instead of leaking into messages, Claude CLI sessions call registered OpenClaw tools by their MCP names, providers that never send response headers time out, agent runs honor the provider's live thinking levels without reloading catalogs, LM Studio refresh discovers new models, llama.cpp setup keeps aliased models and fails when credentials cannot be saved, and guarded model requests fall back to IPv6. (#169728, #159665, #153032, #168282, #168566, #168039, #150262, #168232, #168973, #87763) Thanks @TOMUIV, @kiranvk-2011, @RomneyDa, @obviyus, @jb-lopez, @an2in, and @georgenaz.
  • MiniMax: M3.1 models honor their thinking effort levels, and MiniMax OAuth sign-ins refresh expired tokens instead of failing about two hours after login. (#160089, #160037, #160459, #77467) Thanks @NianJiuZst, @Takhoffman, @php737, @masatohoshino, and @rongtianhua.
  • ACP and compaction: ACP agents keep plugin and OpenClaw tools when acpx is installed as a separate package, and the next turn after native compaction no longer fails while the transcript projection rebuilds. (#164272, #164211, #169583) Thanks @jayzhou2309, @obviyus, and @ajmtrz.
  • Automations: manual runs keep tool access after the starting turn ends, cron no longer holds the state writer while waiting on the host so updates do not fail with a locked database, and a one-shot automation that fails terminally now sends a failure notice instead of disabling silently. (#168037, #168291) Thanks @obviyus.
  • pnpm and platform updates: pnpm 12 Gateway services and openclaw launchers stay on the durable install link after updates, Windows updates accept qualified Scheduled Task account names and no longer roll back when Doctor re-checks ancestry, macOS updates accept protected Bun service commands, and clean service-install refusals stay recoverable. (#170003, #169059, #170131, #169244, #169481, #169563, #170006, #169662, #169658) Thanks @xilopaint, @k-sekula, @RomneyDa, and @jokerman1302-eng.
  • Update recovery: package-activation journals settle after filesystem remounts and device-number drift, retained updater runtimes outside node_modules are reclaimed and never hard-link recovery files, upgrades keep state ownership across the heartbeat transition, and update-time Doctor keeps its child identities after a failed custody binding. (#169410, #170046, #169050, #169352, #170170, #169463, #169915, #169665) Thanks @patrickschiller, @RomneyDa, @SergeiAug, @Warhangel, and @vincentkoc.
  • Update outcomes: updates with a running Gateway no longer record false post-install failures after migration, unsafe Doctor recovery guidance is kept until settled, candidate bookkeeping no longer makes older updaters refuse their config repair, and Doctor cleanup during updates no longer fails with nested-effect errors. (#170130, #169422, #170207, #170195, #170070, #168750) Thanks @angeliti999, @hannesrudolph, and @ezimerman.
  • Update database contention: updates ride out a briefly locked state database, update history writes no longer stall Gateway cron and cleanup, and Doctor recovery captures stay stable while shared-state workers remain open. (#168210, #169283, #170022) Thanks @DonnieFi.
  • Doctor data safety: Doctor keeps OAuth profiles when the legacy auth store is unreadable, refuses retired state before migrating the database, imports retained ACP metadata after archiving legacy session stores, restores a stopped Gateway when cleanup fails, and removes the (multiple) store sentinel registered as an agent database. (#170256, #168959, #170051, #169918, #169759, #169783, #170043, #169764) Thanks @Yolo521-bot, @bautrey, @RomneyDa, and @ourcoms2018.
  • oc-path: openclaw path set refuses malformed UTF-8 before writing, preserving the file's bytes. (#168491) Thanks @wangmiao0668000666 and @Takhoffman.

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.9.89 Stable2026-10-13T10:43:52Z

0.9.89 (2026-10-13)

Sixteen community pull requests: cross-repo member-call resolution (Go, PHP, TypeScript, Kotlin, Objective-C), generic/template type-parameter cleanup (Kotlin, Scala, C++, Swift), an Objective-C enum extractor, interface-dispatch fan-out, a native Pi guard extension, and several resolution/ingest fixes. The AST cache schema is bumped, so the first build after upgrading re-extracts.

  • Feature: cross-repo member calls now finish across a repository boundary for Go, PHP, TypeScript/JS, Kotlin and Objective-C: a call whose receiver type is declared in another repo is parked by name during the single-repo build and bound on merge-graphs/global add against a unique same-named exported type, fail-closed on ambiguity (#3152, #3388, #3393, #3385, #3387, #3389, #3391, #3395, thanks @xiongjianxu).
  • Fix: Go a package's type whose methods span several files now folds into one node instead of fragmenting under _disambiguate_colliding_node_ids, so single-definition guards and receiver typing work (#3399, #3400, thanks @xiongjianxu).
  • Fix: Kotlin / Scala / C++ / Swift generic and template type parameters (class Box<T>, [T], template<typename T>, struct Stack<Element>) no longer mint sourceless stub god-nodes; a real same-named type is still referenced (#4406, #4407, #4408, #4409, thanks @rajatnagda45).
  • Feature: Objective-C NS_ENUM and NS_OPTIONS declarations are now extracted as an enum type plus its members, with the macro forms validated and malformed macros rejected fail-closed (#4400, #4401, thanks @oleksii-tumanov).
  • Feature: dispatch an interface member now links to every implementer, not just one, so a DI chain through a multi-implementer interface resolves; the fan-out is gated to genuine implementers with a correct method match (#3800, #4396, thanks @Goodkamalpandey).
  • Feature: Pi a native Pi guard extension now runs graphify hook-guard before Pi's tool calls and also loads under Oh My Pi, so the hook-guard policy reaches Pi; install/uninstall delegates to pi install/pi remove and the guard fails open (#4378, thanks @andrebrait).
  • Feature: hook a firing read/search nudge now shows the graph nodes behind the nudged call and tightens its wording, so agents act on it instead of treating it as noise; the graph read piggybacks on the already-bounded nudge and is injection-safe and size-capped (#4368, thanks @andrebrait).
  • Fix: resolution a .js import now resolves to a .tsx source when no real .js file exists, matching TypeScript's NodeNext resolution; a real .js still wins (#3486, #4395, thanks @DevChiniwala).
  • Fix: distinct downloaded binary captures (PDFs/images) whose URLs collide to one filename are now kept under numbered names instead of silently overwriting each other, matching the markdown path's de-dup (#4393, #4394, thanks @oleksii-tumanov).

View release →

v0.9.88 Stable2026-10-12T22:19:19Z

0.9.88 (2026-10-12)

Twenty community pull requests: language-extraction and call-resolution fixes (Java, C/C++, Python, Swift, Go, PowerShell, C#, JS/EJS), dedup/shrink-guard hardening, and query/graph.html ergonomics. The AST cache schema is bumped, so the first build after upgrading re-extracts.

  • Feature: Java a call on a chained, new or cast receiver (factory.builder().size(3).build(), new Builder().size(1), ((Builder) o).build()) now resolves through the declared return types on the method chain, binding only on a single unambiguous target (#4306, #4307, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: Java a bare call or field receiver inherited from a supertype in another file now resolves to the nearest declaring supertype (class wins over an interface default; a tie binds nothing), instead of dangling (#4304, #4305, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: C/C++ a quoted cross-directory include (#include "../lib/foo.h", STM32 Core/Src to Core/Inc) now resolves to the right header by a unique longest-shared-directory-prefix match, fail-closed on ties and never guessing for ./, ../, absolute or angle-bracket includes (#4354, #4392, thanks @hopstreax).
  • Fix: Python a @name.setter/.getter/.deleter decorator no longer mints a sourceless stub node (#4380, #4385, thanks @rajatnagda45).
  • Fix: Python a Self (PEP 673) type annotation (-> Self, : Self, typing.Self) no longer mints a sourceless self stub (#4381, #4386, thanks @rajatnagda45).
  • Fix: Swift a Self type annotation no longer mints a sourceless self stub (#4382, #4387, thanks @rajatnagda45).
  • Fix: Go generic type parameters ([T any]) in signatures, struct fields and interface methods no longer mint sourceless t/r stubs (#4383, #4388, thanks @rajatnagda45).
  • Fix: Python a class type-hinted before it is defined (a forward reference) no longer mints a bare stub that collides with and swallows the file node (#4334, #4343, thanks @Mpasha17).
  • Fix: C# extraction now recovers members declared after a verbatim interpolated string that ends in an escaped quote ($@"a""{e}"""), instead of silently dropping the rest of the file; the fix masks only the offending bytes and discards the rewrite unless it re-parses clean (#3376, #3612, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: PowerShell dot-sourced includes with quoted or $PSScriptRoot paths now emit import edges, and script-scope commands (outside any function) are now collected as calls (#4356, #4369, thanks @FabioRueda).
  • Fix: JS/TS a bare or scoped external import is now anchored to the package name rather than the path leaf, so @scope-a/utils and @scope-b/utils stay distinct and lodash/fp/map collapses onto one lodash node (#3084, #3092, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Feature: EJS (.ejs) templates now have an extractor: scriptlet functions/classes, require/import edges, dynamic imports, and <%- include('partial') %> composition edges, instead of a single top-level parse error (#3811, #4338, thanks @nothariharan).
  • Fix: serve a dotted Python query (pkg.mod.func, pkg.mod:func) now resolves to the symbol by module path, fail-closed on a same-file ambiguity, without regressing bare-name lookups (#4366, #4367, thanks @Mpasha17).
  • Fix: dedup now path-qualifies semantic ids that dropped the directory, so a/x.md and b/x.md (both minting a bare x) stay distinct instead of collapsing and losing one node; a reference to the bare id from a third file is left unresolved with a warning rather than guessed (#4281, #4362, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: a semantic node that dedup legitimately folded into its AST twin is now exempt from the shrink guard, so graphify update on a graph that had an LLM semantic ghost is no longer wrongly write-blocked; the exemption is scoped to a strict _origin=="ast" twin at the same path and label, so a genuine partial-build loss is still refused. LLM node/edge field aliases (file/filepath, relationship) are folded, with the canonical field always winning (#4389, #4391, thanks @AndresDiagoM).
  • Fix: install graphify install/uninstall now removes only graphify's own hook entries (keyed on the hook-guard/hook-check command, not a loose "graphify" substring), keeping co-located user hooks and other commands in the same group, and routes uninstall through a backup (#4359, #4373, thanks @nothariharan).
  • Fix: install CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md and sibling files now keep their original line endings (CRLF or LF) across install and uninstall, instead of being normalized to the platform default (#4360, #4372, thanks @nothariharan).
  • Fix: wiki index rows now link to their own article when two communities or god-nodes share a label, instead of both pointing at the first-registered slug and orphaning the second article (#3032, #3033, #3095, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Feature: query --seed starts the traversal at given nodes (ids, labels, callable names or paths) so an external ranker can choose the start set; unresolved seeds are reported and skipped, and an all-unresolved set fails closed rather than falling back to keyword seeds (#4293, #4289, thanks @devkancheti4-design).
  • Feature: graph.html the sidebar now shows a hover tooltip with the full (truncated) legend/search/neighbor name and the legend node count; user text is written via textContent, preserving the embedded-JSON escaping (#4286, #4291, thanks @Hassannzidan).

psmux/psmux ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v3.4.0 Stable2026-10-13T09:38:51Z

psmux v3.4.0

Terminal multiplexer for Windows - tmux alternative for PowerShell and Windows Terminal.

Highlights

  • Signed binaries. Every psmux.exe, pmux.exe and tmux.exe, every installer and the uninstaller inside it are now code signed (Certum, "Open Source Developer Godwin Josh") with a timestamp, so Windows no longer shows them as coming from an unknown publisher.
  • Floating panes with prefix * (new-pane), listed by list-panes, with their own cursor and a confirmation on prefix x.
  • Synchronized output (DECSET 2026). Claude Code and other TUIs that draw a frame at a time no longer flicker or show half drawn frames.
  • The command prompt edits like tmux's. One shared editor for every prompt: C-b, C-f, C-h, C-d, C-u, C-k, C-w, C-t, history on C-p and C-n, and keys the prompt has no use for no longer leak into the pane behind it.
  • Command lists from the CLI run every command in order (psmux new-session -d \; split-window \; ...), including inside if-shell branches, and a trailing ; ends a command the way tmux reads it.
  • Choosers closer to tmux: x asks before it kills, q and C-g close every chooser, + - h l expand and collapse the tree, Page Up and Page Down move by the list height, and another session's windows expand to their panes.
  • tmux parity for show-environment / set-environment and update-environment, set-clipboard, key-table, select-pane -d / -e, case exact session names, and if-shell / run-shell format expansion.
  • Terminals: Alacritty on Windows now gets the VT input route (the prefix works and nothing is typed into the pane at startup), plus fixes for pastes, Escape Escape and mouse handling across Windows Terminal, WezTerm and conhost.
  • Faster and steadier: a warm pool three panes deep for instant splits and windows, sturdier server teardown, and no stranded shells after kill-server.

Special thanks

This release is as much yours as mine. Thank you all for the code, the measurements and the patience:

  • @tanaeakihiko for an extraordinary run of work: the shared prompt editor and C-t (#784, #786), the Alacritty input fix (#788), set-clipboard parity (#772), the chooser kill confirmation (#779), copy mode page scrolling, keyboard selection and goto line (#682, #688, #723), cursor shape restore (#736), prompt cursor and paste routing fixes (#743, #745, #747, #751), the floating pane follow ups (#770) and many more, plus the key by key tmux audits of copy mode, the choosers and the prompts (#694, #776, #782) that drove a large part of this release.
  • @tscodeplus for window-size latest that follows the active client (#663), mouse-drag-enter-copy-mode (#664), a copy mode that works on a snapshot while capture-pane stays live (#671), copy mode yank and resize fixes (#665, #666, #678), CJK and burst paste fixes (#667, #676, #701), reconnect and reader robustness (#710, #737) and a non blocking foreground run-shell (#740).
  • @MattKotsenas for configurable active pane border indicators (#628), centralized render options (#633), OSC 8 hyperlinks in client_termfeatures (#764), split-window -Z (#572), deferred command targets (#587), window style and border background fixes (#617, #624), and Clippy in CI for the pty crate (#591, #595).
  • @darkmirage for select-pane -d / -e (#777) and CLI command lists that run every command in one connection (#780).
  • @maxmya for synchronized output support that ends the flicker next to Claude Code (#781).
  • @Adamkadaban for opt in drag to copy inside mouse aware applications (#575).
  • @neich for drag selections that auto scroll through scrollback at the pane edges (#590).
  • @PhotoAtomic for making the wheel scroll over inline TUIs instead of cycling their prompt history (#548).
  • @BizaNator for the base64 send-paste CLI transport (#584).
  • @airHalfSoundHalf for parking a hidden pane cursor so IME composition follows the caret (#727).
  • @cloudor126 for giving nushell its own dialect when panes are rehomed (#721).
  • @c3nx for keeping the server detached after a console injection (#762).
  • @andrew-tawfeek for refreshing build provenance after source edits and worktree changes (#636).
  • @457shop for the wheel authorization idea in #614, which shaped the fix that landed for #613.

And thank you to everyone who opened issues with careful reproductions and stayed to retest. Every one of them made psmux better.

Downloads

Portable (zip):

Platform File
Windows x64 (Intel/AMD 64-bit) psmux-v3.4.0-windows-x64.zip
Windows x86 (Intel/AMD 32-bit) psmux-v3.4.0-windows-x86.zip
Windows ARM64 (Snapdragon/Surface Pro X) psmux-v3.4.0-windows-arm64.zip

Installer (NSIS setup: kills running instances, adds to PATH, warmup):

Platform File
Windows x64 psmux-v3.4.0-x64-setup.exe
Windows x86 psmux-v3.4.0-x86-setup.exe
Windows ARM64 psmux-v3.4.0-arm64-setup.exe

Installation

Via Scoop (recommended):

scoop bucket add psmux https://github.com/psmux/scoop-psmux
scoop install psmux

Via Chocolatey:

choco install psmux

Via WinGet:

winget install marlocarlo.psmux

Via Cargo:

cargo install psmux

Via PowerShell:

irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/psmux/psmux/master/scripts/install.ps1 | iex

Manual Installation:

  1. Download the zip matching your architecture
  2. Extract to a folder in your PATH
  3. psmux, pmux, and tmux commands will all be available

What's Included

  • psmux.exe - Main executable
  • pmux.exe - Alias
  • tmux.exe - Alias for tmux compatibility

cline/cline ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
cli-v3.0.71CLI v3.0.71 Stable2026-10-13T08:56:25Z
  • Plugins now load inside the hub instead of a separate node or bun process, so the CLI no longer needs a JavaScript runtime on your PATH to run plugins. A plugin that fails to load no longer silently loses its tools: Cline prints a warning naming the plugin and the error, cline config tools --json lists it as a plugin_failure entry, and the new cline hub plugins shows each plugin's status and last error (--reload <plugin> re-imports one without restarting the hub). Set CLINE_PLUGIN_MODE=sandbox to keep the old subprocess behavior
  • --thinking now applies in ACP mode (--acp). Before, ACP sessions always ran with reasoning off
  • cline hook accepts agent_error events. Before, it exited with an error and no response
  • A provider API key set in the environment (such as OPENROUTER_API_KEY) now counts as configured at startup, for providers that read a single environment variable. Before, the TUI asked you to set up the provider anyway
  • With no model chosen, the Cline provider now uses the first model in Cline's recommended list (Claude Sonnet 5.5)
  • Turning reasoning off now works on every provider. Models that can't turn it off keep their default, and level-only Gemini models no longer reject the request with a thinking-budget error
  • Screenshots and other images no longer inflate the token estimate, which made auto-compaction run long before the conversation was near its limit
  • Auto-compaction uses the selected model's context window when the catalog entry has none
  • Scheduled runs: queued runs now start instead of staying pending forever, a run whose model call fails (for example, a retired model) is marked failed with the error, and after downtime only the latest missed occurrence of a schedule runs instead of every one back to back
  • Rule files that are symlinks now load
  • Skills in .claude/skills are now discovered
  • The editor tool no longer receives null in place of the text to replace from some models
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds the AI/ML API, LowRouter, and Prime Inference providers. The v0 provider now offers only its default model, v0-1.5-md, and OpenCode Go uses its new https://opencode.ai/inference/go/v1 endpoint. The Cline free list adds Step 5 Preview. Default models change for 302.AI, CrossModel, Cortecs, DigitalOcean, NEAR AI Cloud, and Ofox (Claude Haiku 5.5), DevPass (LLM Gateway), LLM Gateway, and Meta (Muse Wonder 1.0), Aixy (GPT-6.1 Sol), CloudFerro Sherlock and Moark (GLM 5.3), Ambient, NanoGPT, Neuralwatt, and Novita AI

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/cli-v3.0.70...cli-v3.0.71

sdk/sdk/v0.0.93SDK v0.0.93 Stable2026-10-13T08:26:52Z
  • Plugins load in-process through one registry per process instead of a node/bun subprocess, so the compiled CLI and its hub no longer need a runtime on PATH, and plugins can use the Bun global under the Bun-built hub. CLINE_PLUGIN_MODE=sandbox keeps the subprocess sandbox. The registry wraps import, setup, hooks, tools and commands with timeouts and error capture, and tracks each plugin's state (loading/ready/degraded/failed/disabled) with its last error. A failing plugin no longer silently drops its tools: session.create accepts a plugins policy and returns pluginIssues, later failures reach affected sessions as status notices, the hub answers plugins.list/plugins.status/plugins.reload and broadcasts plugin.status_changed, and the daemon marks a plugin failed instead of shutting down when an uncaught error comes from it. Each session gets its own copy of every plugin module, so plugins that keep per-session state at module level keep working
  • Turning reasoning off now works on every provider, not just a fixed list. Models whose catalog entry has no off option are sent nothing and keep their default, and models without a reasoning capability are skipped. Gemini and Vertex send exact thinkingBudget values only to models that advertise a budget; level-only Gemini models get a thinking level instead (they rejected thinkingBudget). A request never carries both the portable reasoning option and a native provider control (effort, thinking, thinkingConfig, …); the native one wins
  • The cline provider's default model is the first entry of the Cline recommended-models list, falling back to anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5. CLINE_DEFAULT_MODEL_ID moves from Claude Sonnet 5 to Claude Sonnet 5.5, and the openrouter provider default is now anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5 too
  • estimateRequestInputTokens and createTokenEstimator no longer bill base64 image payloads at the text rate. A ~90 KB screenshot counted as ~30k tokens, so a few images triggered auto compaction long before the transcript was near budget. Each image now adds a flat ESTIMATED_TOKENS_PER_IMAGE (1600). New createImageAwareReplacer export in @cline/shared
  • Auto compaction uses the resolved model's context window. When the discovered catalog entry had no limit but the selected-model override did, compaction was handed the bare catalog entry and ran against the wrong budget
  • Scheduled runs: queued runs are claimed correctly under bun:sqlite, which bound the store's named parameters as NULL so the desktop hub never claimed a queued run and it stayed pending with no error. Runs whose agent turn ends with a provider error (such as a retired model id) are now recorded as failed with the error message instead of succeeding silently, and the run report keeps usage and tool calls. Queued cron occurrences superseded by a newer occurrence of the same schedule are cancelled, so a backlog no longer replays every missed slot back to back
  • Rule files that are symlinks are now loaded from config directories instead of skipped. Edits to a link's target are picked up on the next refresh or new session
  • resolveSkillsConfigSearchPaths scans .claude/skills, so skills authored for Claude Code are visible to Cline
  • The editor tool advertises old_text as a plain string. When the schema allowed null, some models sent null instead of the replacement text. Null is still accepted at runtime and treated as omitted
  • Composio connectors: signed-in sessions register Composio's meta tools (search, manage connections, wait for connections, multi-execute), so the agent can find connector tools across toolkits, hand the user a Connect Link for an app that is not connected yet, and run what it found. Connector tools are discovered through meta search; saved desktop connection state no longer supplies model tools. Hosts can supply their own authentication through the new connectorsRequest option (ConnectorsRequest exported from @cline/core), which is also forwarded to scheduled sessions. resolveComposioToolsStatePath is renamed to resolveComposioConnectionStatePath
  • The Cline cloud sign-in helpers in @cline/core accept an AbortSignal, so hosts can cancel a pending sign-in while it is polling
  • Cloud session refreshes update open sessions with titles renamed on the web, and keep renames made locally while a refresh is in flight
  • Refreshed the model catalog (6,572 → 6,862 models). Adds the AI/ML API, LowRouter, and Prime Inference providers. models.dev no longer lists v0, so the v0 provider ships with only its default model, v0-1.5-md. OpenCode Go's base URL moves from https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1 to https://opencode.ai/inference/go/v1. The Cline free list adds Step 5 Preview. Default models change for 302.AI, CrossModel, Cortecs, DigitalOcean, NEAR AI Cloud, and Ofox (Claude Haiku 5.5), DevPass (LLM Gateway), LLM Gateway, and Meta (Muse Wonder 1.0), Aixy (GPT-6.1 Sol), CloudFerro Sherlock and Moark (GLM 5.3), Ambient, NanoGPT, Neuralwatt, and Novita AI

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/sdk/sdk/v0.0.92...sdk/sdk/v0.0.93

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.8.152 Stable2026-10-13T08:03:27Z

HyperFrames v0.8.152

Released on 2026-10-13.

Check and snapshot save each run in its own folder tied to the revision it came from, and renders clean up evidence from old revisions. Project history keeps .hyperframes/video-state.json current after every edit and records which element each edit targeted. Agents sharing a project can refuse to start while another agent's turn is open (--exclusive), end only their own turn (--who), and end safely when no turn is open (--if-open).

Compatibility notes

  • Default snapshots moved from snapshots/ to .hyperframes/artifacts/snapshots/<id>/. Check reports are written to .hyperframes/artifacts/checks/<id>.json. A render also writes .hyperframes/render-outputs.json. Anything still reading snapshots/ will find nothing. #5522

Features

  • CLI: Save check, snapshot and render artifacts per revision (23d1fe5b4, #5522).
  • Studio: Record video state, edit targets and artifact cleanup in project history (893b4f675, #5524).
  • CLI: Guard history turns with --exclusive, --who and --if-open (7c73b3cae, #5518).

Fixes

  • Keyframes: Report unresolved GSAP timing instead of guessing rest poses (8e4de38be, #5526). keyframes --json can return null for start, end, duration, and time when that timing cannot be resolved.
  • CLI: Turn off onnxruntime telemetry for Kokoro synthesis (f25cfbd6b, #5425).

Catalog

  • Catalog: Match preview length to authored compositions (fb4189ca7, #5533).

Internal

  • Inspector: Publish @hyperframes/inspector to npm (f31b3c982, #5530).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.151...v0.8.152

HyperFrames v0.8.151

Released on 2026-10-13.

Standard and high-quality exports preserve graded footage without the colour noise introduced by JPEG frame capture. This release also improves ProRes colours, video cuts and animation seeking.

Features

  • CLI: Check warns when a tween ends just after a later tween on the same property (69f3306415, #5521).
  • Inspector: Element map, live inspection engine and Node runtime (072f53cda9, #5509).
  • Inspector: Query timelines and inspect the live preview (f45788bb6d, #5508).
  • Inspector: Add the component inspector package (73bbd378df, #5507).
  • CLI: Snapshot a time range or one composition (1cd086f7e3, #5519).

Fixes

  • Producer: Stop JPEG frame capture adding colour noise to graded footage (f073281bd9, #5514).
  • CLI: Resolve the render worker's tsx loader from the CLI install (e9216456a5, #5517).
  • Lint: Read a sub-composition's linked stylesheet from the project root (9713e93a52, #5084).
  • Engine: Encode and tag ProRes mov output as BT.709 (c8e105dc92, #5382).
  • Media Use: Transcript-cut video renders without repeated frames or a keyframe warning (1d2aa5240d, #5463).
  • CLI: Say when the Docker image build times out instead of blaming Docker (3bf9035d2a, #5394).
  • Media Use: Keep frame precision on transcript-cut seek boundaries (b71c9f4e1b, #5426).
  • Media Use: Honor telemetryEnabled: false from shared config in optedOut (1577060dc1, #5490).
  • Studio: Splitting a video in an untimed wrapper cuts the video, not the wrapper (aa9413b05e, #5486).
  • Core: An animation playing at its first seek shows the sought time (5e6e58fc9b, #5503).
  • Media: Read ElevenLabs input as UTF-8 (ea7a4e433f, #5469).
  • Media Use: Clean up temporary TTS text directories (73d89b81c7, #5477).
  • Skills: Reject occlusion timeout before verdict (4120042ca3, #5491).

Docs & Examples

  • Lint: Add a reference page for every lint finding code (c3816a121b, #5493).
  • CLI: Document open, catch-up, timeline and history (2973b3c8e4, #5442).
  • Changelog: Weekly digest 2026-10-05–2026-10-12 (86ff462d21, #5497).
  • Changelog: Weekly digest 2026-09-28-2026-10-05 (0e535ac6ab, #5076).

Catalog

  • Catalog: Publish generated catalog (ab06445439, #5500).
  • Registry: Clamp filmed-screen's curve and vignette (37a79e9371, #5501).
  • Registry: Filmed-screen curves, orbits and follows typing by default (3886aaec28, #5499).
  • Catalog: Publish generated catalog (4f941de0da, #5498).
  • Registry: Add filmed-screen component (071ce8044f, #5487).

Internal

  • Core: Share keyframe property defaults between SDK and Studio (b100ff16f3, #5438).

Wei-Shaw/sub2api ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.16Sub2API 0.2.16 Stable2026-10-13T07:20:24Z

AI API Gateway Platform - 将 AI 订阅配额分发和管理

Gemini 支持显式上下文缓存（AI Studio / Vertex / 自建网关上游），并新增 Claude Haiku 5.5 模型适配。

新增功能

  • Claude Haiku 5.5：全链路适配模型注册、推理强度、定价与计费，并接入 Responses 桥接
  • Gemini 显式上下文缓存：支持创建、续期、删除缓存，内置官方存储价并按 Pro / Flash 系列兜底，存储费按 API Key 额度与订阅额度预留校验
  • Gemini Vertex（Agent Platform）：支持 API Key 认证，服务账号支持同步上游模型列表，模型列表与详情接口支持 Vertex 账号
  • Gemini AI Studio：适配新的 AQ. 格式 Key
  • 临时不可调度规则新增作用范围选项：可选择命中后暂停整个账号或只冷却当前模型，未设置的存量规则行为不变
  • 分组名称与描述支持多语言
  • 运维请求详情表新增账号名称列

优化改进

  • GPT-6.1 Sol 订阅账号不再收取长上下文附加费
  • GPT-6.1 Sol 生成上下文默认值提升至 500k
  • Gemini 3.6 Flash 兜底价改为官方优惠价
  • 第三方上游 API Key 与 Vertex 账号不再套用 AI Studio 等级和模拟配额
  • Vertex 显示名改为 Agent Platform (Vertex)
  • Kimi 国际版 Coding 端点（api.kimi.ai）支持配额查询

Bug 修复

  • 修复上游拒绝结构化输入时 Chat 回退重试的问题，并保留 Responses 语义、避免不安全重试
  • 修复 Kimi / DeepSeek / MiniMax 等非 OpenAI 账号被注入 Codex 图像生成桥接导致 400 的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 引用不存在的缓存返回 403 时账号被停用并换号的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 工具结果中的图片丢失问题
  • 修复 Antigravity 图像模型限流影响同账号其他模型的问题
  • 修复显式模型级 401 规则在故障转移、计数、Bedrock 等入口未生效、仍整号置错的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 原生路由网关本地错误在运维中的归类问题
  • 修复 Gemini 授权链接生成失败时错误信息丢失的问题
  • 修复同步上游模型失败时未显示后端错误原因的问题
  • 修复风控审核范围中残留已删除分组的问题
  • 修复分组 RPM 覆盖加载过期数据的问题，并拒绝非正数的倍率修改
  • 修复支付二维码倒计时漂移、用量倒计时切回标签页不刷新的问题
  • 修复渠道监控 v2 设置无法添加缺失平台、自动刷新不更新缓存可用性的问题
  • 修复运维自定义时间范围未按本地时区初始化的问题
  • 修复下拉选择框在输入法组字时键盘输入被打断的问题
  • 修复账号列表中分组名称显示不全的问题

📥 Installation

Docker:

# Docker Hub
docker pull weishaw/sub2api:0.2.16

# GitHub Container Registry
docker pull ghcr.io/wei-shaw/sub2api:0.2.16

One-line install (Linux):

curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Wei-Shaw/sub2api/main/deploy/install.sh | sudo bash

Manual download: Download the appropriate archive for your platform from the assets below.

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.21.11 Stable2026-10-13T06:50:11Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.11

0.21.11 is a patch release for the frozen range v0.21.10..7e0b5f2330b9d85c5fdb9709ec0104e099836f4c (3 commits, 2 files, +68/−5): native agent TOMLs no longer retain stale inherited model providers.

Highlights

  • Fresh provider inheritance (#3781, fixes #3779): native agent TOMLs omit model_provider when it only mirrors the root config.toml default, so changing the provider no longer leaves stale agent configuration or requires omx setup --force. Explicit per-agent model overrides remain supported.

Fixes and compatibility

  • Avoid baking inherited root model_provider values into native agent TOMLs; preserve explicit per-agent overrides (#3781, fixes #3779).
  • Patch release with no intentional breaking CLI or package-layout changes.

Validation evidence

Frozen candidate green on dev CI; local build, typecheck, and relevant tests recorded.

Full readiness evidence: docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.11.md.

Merged PR inventory

#3781 @Yeachan-Heo fix: do not bake model_provider into agent TOMLs for default inheritance (fixes #3779)

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

Inventory

The reproducible range is recorded in artifacts/release-0.21.11/inventory.md.

v0.21.10 Stable2026-10-13T00:18:12Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.10

0.21.10 is a patch release for the frozen range v0.21.9..72fee0236cf8a4b45e6dd9b3d826871f402b84ae (16 commits, 15 files, +1067/−82): astra-defaults evaluation infrastructure hardening, configuration merge robustness, and npm registry-visibility diagnostics.

Highlights

  • TOML configuration merge robustness (#3777, fixes #3776): multi-line arrays in the [tui] configuration section are now correctly preserved during config merge operations, preventing loss of array elements and enabling complex TUI customization without manual repair.
  • Astra-defaults evaluation infrastructure (#3655, #3765, #3772): the astra-defaults RunRecord collector gains base URL support for flexible test fixture hosting and implements repository cloning with fixture context, enabling live cost and quality evaluation across model configurations. Collector is now hermetic with bounded Codex v0.162+ JSON-line parsing and includes CI integration (#3768).
  • npm registry-visibility diagnostics (#3771): registry-visibility checks now include extended timeout windows and improved diagnostics to surface transient publication issues earlier and support troubleshooting of npm registry visibility failures in CI pipelines.

Fixes and compatibility

  • Fix preservation of multi-line arrays in TOML [tui] configuration sections during merge operations (#3777, fixes #3776).
  • Harden astra-defaults collector for hermetic operation with correct handling of Codex v0.162+ JSON-line output format and repository cloning (#3772, #3774).
  • Extend npm registry-visibility check window and improve diagnostics for faster visibility confirmation (#3771).
  • Patch release with no intentional breaking CLI or package-layout changes.

Validation evidence

Frozen candidate green on dev CI; local build, typecheck, and full compiled test suite recorded.

Full readiness evidence: docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.10.md.

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

Inventory

The reproducible range is recorded in artifacts/release-0.21.10/inventory.md.

Full Changelog: v0.21.9...v0.21.10

coreyhaines31/marketingskills ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.11.27v2.11.27 — Analysis rules from the Magister benchmark (#573). Four rules, each written into the ski… Stable2026-10-13T05:34:46Z

  • Analysis rules from the Magister benchmark (#573). Four rules, each written into the skill and mirrored in its description so hosted agents see them before opening the file:

    • ad-creative (2.10.0 → 2.11.0): a new Test Hypotheses That Can Lose section. A hypothesis names the metric, the comparison, and the expected direction.
    • copywriting (2.1.0 → 2.2.0): claims must be supportable, and a declined claim names the evidence that would unlock it.
    • analytics (2.0.3 → 2.1.0): compute metrics from row-level data, and divide rates only over matching closed windows.
    • marketing-plan (1.2.1 → 1.2.2): AARRR stage metrics count only cohorts whose window has closed.

    ad-creative also notes that Magister can run its data-pull step (#567), with a disclosure that Magister is the maintainer's product and alternatives for running the pull yourself.

  • influencer-marketing (1.1.2 → 1.2.0) and social (2.3.3 → 2.3.4): Telegram channel ads (#785 by @Courier-Britva). The new references/telegram-channels.md covers how placements are bought, a seven-check vetting routine based on views per subscriber, and pricing by CPM and deletion window. Contributed by the team behind TGScope, which is disclosed; its benchmarks come from TGScope's own studies and are labeled as directional.

  • Tools:

    • rowbound guide (#500, closes #498): an open-source, self-hosted alternative to Clay that runs enrichment waterfalls from a Google Sheet, with a CLI and MCP.
    • AIsa joins as a ◆ Skill Partner in SEO tools (#783).
    • Across the CLIs, rejected HTTP requests now exit 1 (#713), and invalid flags fail with structured errors before any request is sent (#732), both by @rudycelekli.

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 50 skills. Claude web target: 50 skills with reviewed short descriptions.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

Web omissions (also recorded in claude-web-manifest.json):

  • None.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v13.37.0 Stable2026-10-13T03:07:09Z

This release fixes the broken 13.36.1 marketplace update. After that update, the worker, the MCP search server and the server runtime failed to start, so memory capture stopped. Upgrading is strongly recommended.

Fixes

  • claude-mem starts again after the 13.36.1 marketplace update (#4728, #4729). A change merged after the 13.36.1 release committed the built plugin files with leftover git stash conflict markers. The plugin installs from main, so auto-updated installs got those files under the same 13.36.1 version number. The worker failed with error: Unexpected <<, mcp-search closed its connection, and memory capture stopped once the older worker exited. The files are now rebuilt from clean source. The npm package claude-mem@13.36.1 was published before that change and was not affected. Thank you @nashsibanda for the rebuild and @hirohisatanigawa for the detailed report.
  • Worker startup waits out a locked database instead of failing (#4725). The schema-writer check added in 13.36.0 ran before the connection's busy timeout was set. A worker starting while another process was opening the same database failed in about 1 ms with database is locked, and after two failures it was not restarted. The check now waits for the lock.

Changed

  • Memory keeps capturing on your Anthropic plan when your selected provider can't serve (#4727). CLAUDE_MEM_QUOTA_FALLBACK_PROVIDER now defaults to claude. Settings files that saved "" get the new default too. The fallback now also covers a provider that is still rate limited or over capacity after its retries, is down, or returns the same batch twice with no usable output. Claude is used only when a Claude Code login or an Anthropic key is available. Memory moves back to your provider once it answers again. To wait for your provider instead, set CLAUDE_MEM_QUOTA_FALLBACK_PROVIDER to off.

Release safety

  • CI now checks the committed plugin files before it rebuilds them. It fails on merge-conflict markers anywhere in plugin/ and runs node --check on every committed plugin script (#4729). Before this, CI rebuilt the bundles first and tested the rebuilt copies, which hid the broken committed ones.

If you used the 13.35.0 workaround

If you followed the workaround in #4728 (auto-update turned off and the plugin pinned to 13.35.0), turn autoUpdate back on for the thedotmack marketplace and restart Claude Code to move to 13.37.0.

Full diff: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v13.36.1...v13.37.0

View release →

v13.36.1 Stable2026-10-12T21:01:07Z

A patch release that closes three issues found in review of 13.36.0, plus eight community fixes. Upgrading is recommended.

Privacy and data safety

  • <private> content no longer leaves the hook in server runtime (#4721). In server runtime, file edits, tool inputs and outputs, and session-start prompts were sent to the server with <private> sections still inside, and the server kept them in the raw event record (they were removed only later, when the model prompt was built). Private sections are now removed before anything leaves the hook, and the server removes them again before it stores events or session metadata. Worker runtime (the default) was not affected.
  • Older and newer workers can no longer race on a database upgrade (#4724). The check that stops an older claude-mem from opening a database a newer version upgraded now reads and records the schema writer inside one SQLite write lock, before any migration runs. Before, two workers opening the database at the same moment could both pass the check.
  • A cancelled Chroma connection can no longer release a newer connection's lock (#4722). Each Chroma connection now owns its writer-lock token and releases only that lock, so a reconnect during shutdown can't leave the new sidecar unlocked and allow a second writer on the same store. Process-table parsing for the orphan sweep now has fixture tests.

Community fixes — thank you @rudycelekli

  • Integration ids such as constructor no longer resolve through the alias table (#4712)
  • Postgres numeric settings accept only whole positive numbers; values like 5ms fall back to the default (#4713)
  • Gemini retry hints too large to represent are ignored, so a key isn't parked indefinitely (#4714)
  • The billing-type file is read correctly when it starts with a UTF-8 BOM (#4715)
  • API-key settings arrays keep only string entries (#4716)
  • A malformed part in a Gemini response no longer discards the whole answer (#4717)
  • Refused connections are recognized inside wrapped errors, so recovery kicks in (#4718)
  • Hook launchers honor the environment opt-out flags before starting Node or Bun (#4719)

Full diff: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v13.36.0...v13.36.1

View release →

v13.36.0 Stable2026-10-12T18:12:46Z

A community release: nearly every change since v13.35.0 came from outside contributors and is merged under their own name. Thank you all.

Highlights

  • Memories are no longer dropped when the provider says no. An empty Anthropic credit balance, an organization blocking subscription access, or a local model server that is down now pauses the observer and keeps the work instead of discarding it (@Mola-maker). When the observer stream ends early, its batch is handed back for reprocessing (@shantanukhanwalkar).
  • Windows home folders with non-ASCII names work again. Bun and Claude are found when your user folder name contains Korean, Japanese or other non-ASCII characters (@akushonkamen).
  • Hooks don't report your project's tsconfig as errors. This affected React Native and Expo projects (@Totopo27).
  • The viewer saves only what you changed, so an old settings tab can't undo a change you made elsewhere (@Cassian433).
  • Search improvements:
    • Correct paging (@junnhwan).
    • Chinese, Japanese, Korean and Thai words are found inside sentences (@rudycelekli).
    • Long natural-language queries fall back to any-term matching when no memory matches every word (@devkancheti4-design).
  • Chroma reliability:
    • No more stalls after a reboot on large stores (@akushonkamen).
    • Orphaned chroma-mcp processes that hold your data directory are cleaned up safely before starting, so they no longer cause lock fights (@akushonkamen).

New

  • Qoder plugin support (@shouldnotappearcalm).
  • Screenpipe skill for searching meeting and screen history and saving it to memory (@EzraEllette).
  • Atlas Cloud preset for the OpenAI-compatible provider (@binyangzhu000-sudo).
  • CLAUDE_MEM_OPENROUTER_TEMPERATURE setting (@aeneasr).
  • CLAUDE_MEM_SEMANTIC_INJECT_FORMAT=index: compact per-prompt memory injection (@ktdrv).
  • CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_STATE_ENABLED=false to turn off the work-state to-do rule (@noesis-bot).
  • CLAUDE_MEM_TOOL_USES_RETENTION_DAYS: opt-in pruning of the raw tool log. The default keeps everything (@Montinou).
  • Duplicate review: record a "distinct" or "dismissed" verdict on duplicate candidates (@Montinou).
  • Viewer: a Claude auth method control in settings (@akushonkamen).
  • Server runtime: idempotent memory uploads (@escooterclinic).

Fixes

  • Capture and sessions:
    • Original capture timestamps are kept for delayed events (@junnhwan).
    • Stale session-end events are retired (@akushonkamen).
    • Warnings are logged before queued work is discarded (@Boulea7).
    • Tool calls whose input is null are captured, logging recovers when the log folder appears later, and every shutdown step runs (@rudycelekli).
    • Search triggers are restored after database upgrades (@akushonkamen).
    • Fewer SQLite warnings on macOS (@CCCLL).
  • Database safety: the database records the newest schema version that has written to it, and an older claude-mem refuses to open a database a newer one has upgraded (@akushonkamen).
  • Memory import and notes:
    • Restored memories attach to existing sessions (@junnhwan).
    • Native note revisions survive title dedup (@junnhwan).
    • Explicit notes keep their platform source in the search index (@junnhwan).
    • Notes that start with ---- are no longer mangled (@akushonkamen).
  • Search robustness: malformed file lists, plain-text concepts, folder search against absolute paths, consistent date parsing, and better patterns for native search commands (@rudycelekli).
  • Installers and integrations:
    • Cursor configs with a BOM and commented Codex TOML are read correctly, and npm can no longer hang the installer (@rudycelekli).
    • Worker ports and IPv6 addresses are honored everywhere, and the uvx path can be set in settings (@Ravencloned).
  • Providers and streaming:
    • OpenCode Go session header (@Cassian433).
    • Sturdier streamed replies, emoji never split when long output is trimmed, and Zstandard transcripts keep their text intact (@rudycelekli).
  • Server runtime:
    • Fresh installs no longer get 403 errors (@DeryFerd).
    • File edits reach the server, and CLAUDE_MEM_SKIP_TOOLS is honored in server mode (@DeryFerd).
    • Stuck jobs are reclaimed (@escooterclinic).
    • No stray local database (@vinay121314).
    • Leaner prompts (@rich-murphey).
    • Postgres keeps literal __proto__ keys in stored JSON (@rudycelekli).
    • Stricter Redis settings (@rudycelekli).
  • Sync, Chroma and duplicates:
    • Prompts from other devices are linked (@necatisozer).
    • Turning Chroma off is fully honored (@CCCLL).
    • Fewer Chroma rewrites at startup (@Montinou).
    • Accent-aware duplicate detection (@rudycelekli) and a corrected similarity threshold (@udsy19).
  • Viewer:
    • "Select all" in the Logs drawer works (@DrDLyuan).
    • The context preview opens on a project that has data (@rudycelekli).

Project

  • main is now the only branch. core-dev and community-edge are retired, so please open pull requests against main.

View release →

Mostly fixes for the level you pick. It now survives context compaction instead of falling back to the default, and on macOS the hooks can no longer lose their output, so the ruleset always arrives.

Also in here: the Cursor installer leaves hooks.json files it doesn't understand alone and no longer removes your own hooks with a similar name, OpenCode doesn't get stuck in review anymore and supports /ponytail default, pi completes /ponytail arguments, and OMP starts new sessions at the default level. Thanks to everyone who sent fixes!

v5.1.1 Stable2026-10-12T23:18:11Z

A fix release. The big one is the codebase map from 5.0: a repo could slip its own text into the hidden session context, and a symlink in the repo could hang the session start until the ruleset never arrived. Both are fixed, and the map now has a one-second limit, so the rules always load.

Also in here: hooks work again when a project pins an old Node, three fixes for OpenCode 2 (compaction cache, command arguments, mention highlights), and /ponytail-debt now skips everything in .gitignore. Thanks to everyone who sent reports and fixes this week.

studio-v2.38.0Reasonix Studio v2.38.0 Stable2026-10-13T01:43:36Z

本版加入从任意回复分支会话、会话批量操作、模型显示别名和 MCP 工具定义变更审批，并修复长对话里拖选文字卡死、重载后丢失撤销入口等问题。

新增

  • 可以从任意一条回复分支出一个独立的新会话 (#12770)
  • 会话行上有快速归档和删除；可以多选本地会话做批量操作 (#12775, #12807)
  • 把文件夹拖到侧边栏即可添加为项目，项目右键菜单可以打开项目操作 (#12776, #12324 by @KHG420)
  • 从上下文环和检查器就能压缩上下文 (#12777)
  • 对话导航旁显示当前计划 (#12803)
  • 浏览器面板新增宽屏模式，打开时仍能看到对话；浏览器页面可以逐个或批量关闭 (#11762, #12806)
  • 文件面板右键增加「复制路径」和「复制引用」，名称里含冒号或以反斜杠结尾而无法表示成引用时会禁用后者 (#12818, #12842 by @XTLine)
  • 可以给每个模型设置显示别名；添加来源前可以批量检测模型 (#12821, #12835)
  • 模型偏好里可以设置联网搜索使用的模型 (#11761)
  • MCP 服务器的工具定义变更后，需要重新批准才会启用 (#12829)
  • 回退之后对话顶部显示持久的撤销提示，可以关闭，重载后仍在 (#12831, #12559 by @XTLine)
  • 可以在右键菜单里引用选中的回复文字 (#12836)
  • 发送快捷键成为统一的偏好设置 (#12837)
  • 反馈表单里可以直接打开日志文件夹；反馈对话折叠显示并保留未读状态 (#12768, #12802)
  • 正在使用的版本被撤回时，应用会告知用户 (#12769)
  • 工作台「+」按钮会显示启动器，从导航打开聚焦会话的文件 (#12826 by @XTLine, #12832)

变更

  • 会话标签页改为按需开启 (#12841)
  • 项目里置顶的会话排在最前并带标记 (#12845)
  • 默认浅色主题的层次重新调整；视图标签与页面色调一致 (#12793, #12782)
  • 对话壁纸下的文字光晕可以配置 (#12839)
  • 当前使用的模型在输入框的选择器里排第一 (#12801)
  • 手机窄屏上未输入时的输入框更紧凑，并保留分支标识 (#12800, #12813)
  • 左侧图标栏的图标与对应设置分区一致；设置页打开时侧边栏开关仍可用 (#12753, #12798)
  • 首屏改用 1.9 MB 的核心字体子集；对话导航条每个可区分位置只画一个标记 (#12766, #12767)
  • 多个会话共用一个工作区时，写入改用乐观租约 (#12772)
  • 没有声明检查的项目里，写过文件的未识别命令不再免除验证 (#12848 by @boscocp)

修复

  • 长对话里拖选文字会卡死：选区经过的远处块改为按对话原文保留，1200 轮对话里跨屏选择从数十秒降到约 0.1 秒；这些中间块复制出的是对话原文，不再带界面标签 (#12850)
  • 一个超过 128 个字符的 MCP 工具名会让整个会话的每次请求失败：现在只缩短工具名部分 (#12849)
  • 密集的时间线信号可以逐个查看 (#12815)
  • 压缩对话时转录区不再跳动 (#12783)
  • 停放的浏览器页面再次显示时会重新附着，不再留下空白 (#12778)
  • Windows 上替换插件目录时不再重命名被监视的目录 (#11822)
  • 反馈里正确报告 Windows 11 的产品线 (#12734 by @jf200s)
  • 没有任何对话的文件夹现在返回空的会话列表 (#12744 by @jipeng6036-del)
  • 终端界面里补全菜单中按 Esc 会同时停止正在运行的回合 (#12761 by @xxoingr)
  • 记忆改名时先下线旧名再发布；归档记忆恢复失败后可以重试，索引与归档保持一致 (#12785 by @chuiwenwei, #12816 by @chuiwenwei, #12819 by @chuiwenwei)
  • 自动召回的记忆在轨迹里记录是被条数还是字符预算截掉的 (#12843 by @thehui13)

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.32 Stable2026-10-13T00:15:04Z

5.1.32 is the release that 5.1.31 was meant to be: 5.1.31 never reached npm. Two checks in the release pipeline stopped it before any package was published, one on the macOS Intel desktop engine and one on the Linux musl builds. omo itself did not change between the two, so every entry below is new to anyone upgrading from 5.1.30. The notarization check now waits for Apple's ticket to reach Gatekeeper, and the musl smoke no longer reports an exited host supervisor as a leak. (#10081, #10082)

omo runs on senpi 2026.10.12, and long sessions stop growing with every shell command. A finished bash session used to keep its whole headless terminal screen (10,000 lines of scrollback plus replay history, about 20 MB for a 4,000-line output) until the 32-session limit evicted it, so a busy session or task host grew by that much per command; it now keeps only its final visible screen and its bounded output, and a live terminal's replay history is stored as flat strings instead of about 31 bytes per character. After an accepted compaction, prompts, retries and resumes use the compacted context, so a session that fits is no longer refused with "Context remains above the compaction threshold" or compacted twice. When one provider account runs out of quota, its other models are skipped, compaction runs on a model that can still answer, and the error names the provider, the reset time and the /model way out instead of raw 429 JSON. ChatGPT-subscription GPT-6.1 Sol, Fast and Ultrafast get a 500,000-token context window. Web search uses the active model's own server-side search first on routes where it was tested live, and falls back once to the standalone search if that fails. Prompt-cache prewarm is now opt-in (promptCache.keepAlive.enabled) and starts only on your first edit, never on session start or resume. RPC clients gain eval_cells.list/peek/stop, and a shared host that is shutting down refuses new sessions with host_stopping instead of adopting them. Thanks to @MoerAI and @Bearmancer. (senpi#3171, senpi#3170, senpi#3137, senpi#3062, senpi#3131, senpi#3150, senpi#3122, senpi#3140, senpi#3072, senpi#3059, senpi#3101, senpi#3055) (#10069)

The Recommended model profile is gone: with no model profile set, OmO starts on Daily · Normal. A fresh OmO Desktop or headless session with no model_profile applies Daily · Normal (Claude Opus 5.5, then Kimi K3, then GLM 5.3). If none of those is connected, it uses the first lane that is: Daily · Heavy, Geeky · Normal, then Geeky · Heavy, so a ChatGPT-only setup starts on Geeky · Normal. A config that still says model_profile: "recommended" gets the same default and one log line; it is never an error, and omo.json is not rewritten. A model_profiles.recommended you defined yourself still applies. The interactive TUI is unchanged. Requested by @code-yeongyu. (#10029)

Session tools find sessions kept in OpenCode's older storage, and show the real error instead of [object Object]. A session that exists only in OpenCode's legacy JSON storage is listed, but the SQLite server answers NotFoundError for it; session_search, session_list, session_read and session_info now read such a session from the older storage instead of failing. Any other error reads as its name and message, and when session_search or session_list (without a date filter) still cannot read a session, it skips it and names it at the end instead of failing the whole call. Thanks to @crazyrabbit0 for the reproduction. (#5854, #10041)

Ultrawork tracks task-owned worktrees and runtime caches when they are created. Cleanup guidance now scopes cache paths before task commands, requires path-specific todos and removal receipts, and preserves worktrees still needed by open PRs without wiping shared caches. Native full and compact directives, Codex, and OpenCode variants carry the same lifecycle; co-loaded workflows refer to it instead of repeating it. (#9951, #9953) Computer use can list and choose menu commands on Linux. On X11 and Wayland, menus.items and menus.select walk a window's menu bar through AT-SPI and run the chosen item's own activation action, without focusing or raising the window. The request is bound to the exact window you named, so a same-titled sibling window of the same app is refused rather than used, and ambiguous, disabled or submenu labels are refused before anything runs. Selecting a menu command also no longer crashes GTK apps on Ubuntu 22.04. (#9651, #9994)

Computer use can take a screenshot and read a window's accessibility tree in one step. observe() on a window returns both together, and a failed or cancelled observation normally leaves the previous screenshot and accessibility references in place. (#9651)

Computer use can zoom into part of a screenshot at full detail without changing where clicks land. zoom(region) on the desktop, a window or a display captures that region again at native resolution, while later clicks and typing still use the coordinates of the last full screenshot. (#9651)

Computer use can list and choose menu commands on Windows. menus.items and menus.select read a window's classic menu bar directly, or its UI Automation menu when it has none, and run the chosen command without keyboard input. Elevated windows, ambiguous or disabled labels, submenus, and menus that change mid-request are refused before anything runs; apps that take focus while they act are refused in the background and work with delivery: "foreground". (#9651, #9991)

Computer use screenshots the monitor you are working on by default, and computer.display(id) reaches any other one. An unset computer.display now captures the active monitor, the one the focused window mostly sits on, instead of compositing every display; "all" and display ids work as before. computer.display("all" | "active" | id) gives a screenshot and input handle for one display without changing the session default, and computer.displays() lists every monitor. (#9651, #9995)

OpenCode requests now follow the selected model's live capabilities instead of a stale model cache. When OpenCode passes the live model to chat.params, its variants, thinking support, temperature support and output limit take precedence over the cached provider list, and the SDK's provider.info.id provider context is accepted alongside provider.id; calls that carry only model IDs keep the cached and bundled fallback. Thanks to @MoerAI. (#10001)

background_task.taskCleanupDelayMs now also sets how long finished background tasks stay retrievable. A delay longer than 30 minutes is no longer cut short; leaving it unset keeps the existing timing. Thanks to @imadal1n. (#9984)

The MCP OAuth callback server answers errors in plain text. Its not-found, provider-error and missing-parameter responses are now sent as text/plain; charset=utf-8 with X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff, so an error_description in the callback URL is shown as text and never rendered as HTML. MCP sign-in currently runs through a second callback server that never echoed that text, so nothing you see changes; consolidating the two is tracked separately. Thanks to @niStee. (#6464, #9979)

OpenCode task delegation and team creation now honor claude_code.agents: false, so disabled Claude Code agents fail fast instead of being selected. Thanks to @cynkai. (#8744, #8802)

Delegated OpenCode sessions show their task category in the title. Background, synchronous and fallback-created child sessions prefix the category while keeping the description and agent suffix; tasks without a category keep their existing title. (#6854, #7256)

OpenCode runtime fallback can switch models after a provider content-filter error. When fallback is enabled and alternate models are configured, a blocked response advances to a different model; the existing attempt limit and chain exhaustion still stop retries. (#6796, #7257)

Memory facts are extracted only after you type a prompt. Pending facts no longer start a model call when a session starts, rebinds, resumes or forks; extraction waits for a real prompt of yours in a top-level session. It runs on memory.facts.model, a configured quick chain or the session's own model, and with none of those available the queue stays pending without recording a failure. (#9922, #9954)

Naming a ulw-* skill no longer switches on ultrawork. ulw-plan, ulw-research, ulw-execute and ulw-loop in a prompt leave ultrawork off, while a bare ulw or ultrawork still turns it on. (#6876, #7255)

Tests now catch environment leaks at the file that causes them. A test file that leaves process.env changed fails with the names of the changed keys, and the next file starts from a clean environment. Thanks to @Dante-dan. (#9390, #9469)

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
mobile-android-v0.0.55Orca Mobile Android mobile-android-v0.0.55 Prerelease2026-10-13T00:07:49Z

Thank you for using Orca mobile! ❤️

Over-the-air updates

The Orca app on your phone now gets most of its screens and features from the desktop it's paired with, instead of having them built into the app. When you update Orca on your computer, the phone picks up the new version the next time it connects.

  • New features sooner. Phone improvements ship with each desktop release. You don't have to wait for an App Store or Play Store update.
  • Phone and desktop always match. The phone runs the version your desktop serves, so features and fixes line up and nothing breaks because one side is newer.
  • Fewer app updates. You only need to update the app itself occasionally.

You'll see a short "Downloading the latest app" screen the first time you connect to a computer, and again after you update Orca there. After that, the app opens right away.

What's new

  • When the app downloads the latest version from your computer, a progress bar shows how far along it is, and downloads finish faster.
  • Grok, OpenCode, OMP and Pi chats now open on the phone, and chat visuals and subagents show inline like on desktop.
  • Messages you send while an agent is working wait above the composer and go out in order. A message that couldn't be delivered is marked "Not sent" and goes back to your draft.
  • Answer an agent's questions with number keys.
  • SSH hosts show their connection health on the workspace list, and the list follows the desktop's pinned-workspace setting.
  • Videos and music files play in file previews.
  • Pairing links are more secure, and scanning a new code replaces an old pairing.
  • Lower memory use, smoother chat scrolling on Android, and more reliable terminals.

Most phone features come from your desktop, so update Orca on your computer to get everything. See the desktop release notes for the full list.

View release →

different-ai/openwork ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.18.58OpenWork v0.18.58 Stable2026-10-13T00:00:43Z

Run OpenWork Web on your own self-hosted deployment

  • Self-host OpenWork Web and team tools on AWS. For self-hosters: run OpenWork Web with sandboxed browser workspaces, and deploy Slack, Workbot, and Automations that run without a desktop. AWS deployments can use existing networks and load balancers, and can connect to an existing cluster or runner fleet.
  • Connect OpenCode 2 to your organization. If you use OpenWork from OpenCode: the opencode-openwork plugin brings in your organization's AI Gateway models, tools, and skills after sign-in, without the desktop app. You can also upgrade desktop chats to OpenCode 2 with a backup first; its conversations can use the built-in browser and discover connected tools reliably.
  • Admins get clearer controls and reporting. Organization Analytics is back, with restored usage and workflow history where available. When enabled, admins can also report on skills, plugins, and connectors, set member, admin, and team permissions, and choose a default model for cloud agents; Audit Logs now cover more organization activity.
  • Chat and daily work recover more smoothly. Pasted text and images stay at the cursor, reopened Apps load their data, migrated chats with a missing agent can answer again, and OpenWork keeps the Working status visible during quiet stretches. Ollama keeps multiple selected models and their thinking choices; Drive uploads respect your chosen Google account.
  • Calendar, Workbot, and connections do more. When Calendar features are enabled, add or edit Automations alongside meetings; Workbot can recover stopped tasks. When enabled, admins can check when connections were last verified, and compatible MCP clients can subscribe to remote-session updates.
  • Some familiar pages and searches have changed. Organization dashboards and admin-assigned dashboards in Desktop are gone, while personal dashboards remain. “Search sessions” now searches session titles and workspace names, not message text; for organizations with OpenWork Models, Auto is replaced in the model picker by GPT-6 Luna.

Full changelog: https://openworklabs.com/docs/changelog · Compare
