earendil-works/pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.2.0 Stable2026-10-12T23:03:53Z

New Features

  • Per-key OpenRouter model lists: with an OpenRouter key, Pi lists only the models that key may use, with the key's context size, max output, and prices, including regional endpoints set in models.json. See OpenRouter.
  • Published configuration schemas: add $schema to settings.json, models.json, keybindings, and themes for editor validation and completion. See Settings, Models, Keybindings, and Themes.
  • Clipboard copies over remote sessions: PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52=1 sends copied text to a remote client's clipboard when Pi cannot detect the remote session, such as a multiplexer attached from another machine. See Pi Process Configuration.
  • Codemode improvements: tool declarations show input constraints such as integer, minimum/maximum, and default, and codemode rows show elapsed and total time. See Codemode.
  • before_agent_start for extension-triggered runs: runs started by pi.sendMessage(..., { triggerTurn: true }) go through the same setup as prompts. See Events and concurrency.

Breaking Changes

  • Published generated configuration schemas for models, settings, keybindings, and themes at https://pi.dev/schemas/*.schema.json. The theme schema moved to https://pi.dev/schemas/theme.schema.json; themes loaded during startup now receive the same schema validation as later reloads. Update theme $schema references, define reusable colors under vars, and remove unsupported metadata (#9880, #10751 by @christianklotz)
  • Changed before_agent_start to also fire for runs started by pi.sendMessage(..., { triggerTurn: true }) while idle. BeforeAgentStartEvent is now a union on messageType: "user" events carry prompt and images, and "custom" events carry the starting message and no prompt. Messages returned by handlers are added to both kinds of run, and pending deliverAs: "nextTurn" messages are delivered with the custom-message run. Handlers that should act only on user prompts must check event.messageType === "user" (#10267, #5581, #10739 by @davidbrai)
  • Changed the session to count as busy while a run starts, including during before_agent_start handlers: ctx.isIdle() is false, pi.sendMessage() queues into the starting run instead of starting a second run that failed with "Agent is already processing", pi.sendUserMessage() requires deliverAs, and an abort requested meanwhile aborts the run once it starts (#10739 by @davidbrai)
  • Changed pi mcp list --json to report resources: true for servers that offer resources instead of resources and resourceTemplates counts; Pi no longer lists resources when connecting (#10526)

Added

  • Added reachesTools to tool definitions, so tools that reach other tools (such as codemode and tool_search) declare which tools a call may reach; the MCP extension uses it to wait for the servers a call needs and to decide whether a replacement codemode or tool_search tool can reach MCP tools
  • Added aliases to tool namespaces; codemode's describeNamespace() and the namespace option of searchTools() match them, and MCP namespaces use the server name
  • Added per-key OpenRouter model lists: with an OpenRouter key, Pi lists only the chat models that key may use, with the key's context size, max output, and prices, including guardrails and regional endpoints set as the base URL in models.json. The list is saved and used offline (#10353, #10672 by @davidbrai)
  • Added PI_CLIPBOARD_OSC52=1 to send copied text to the terminal with OSC 52 after the local clipboard write, so copies reach a remote client's clipboard when Pi cannot detect the remote session, such as multiplexer attaches from another machine or remote IDE daemons (#10826, #10835, #10114)
  • Added elapsed time while a codemode script runs and its total time once finished to the codemode tool row, like bash rows (#10708)

Changed

  • Changed the showHardwareCursor setting to use only the terminal cursor instead of also drawing Pi's reverse-video cursor
  • Changed codemode tool declarations to show input constraints such as integer, minimum/maximum, length, pattern, and default as @ tags on property comments, so scripts see them without the tool repeating them in descriptions (#10707)
  • Changed codemode declarations of MCP tools to spell out the result type (content, structuredContent, isError) instead of referring to CallToolResult<T>, which describeTool(), searchTools(), and ALL_TOOLS showed without defining it; the codemode description no longer includes the shared MCP types
  • Changed serializeConversation() (compaction, branch summaries, bug reports) to omit thinking blocks; replayed thinking is often not in the live context, so including it could make a summarization request larger than the context it summarizes and fail

Fixed

  • Fixed codemode ignoring a // @options: line that is not the first line of the script, which ran the script without the requested timeout_ms; the call now fails with an error before the script runs (#10631)
  • Fixed Mistral streams being aborted mid-generation (e.g. during long thinking) after the request timeout; the timeout now applies only to waiting for response headers (#10609)
  • Fixed a symlinked AGENTS.md in a git worktree nested inside its main repo causing no project context file to load (#10681)
  • Fixed the code mode tool description claiming every failed tool call rejects; a tool that declares an output schema resolves with its value on failure too (e.g. bash with a nonzero exit)
  • Fixed runs started by pi.sendMessage(..., { triggerTurn: true }) removing system prompt sections added in before_agent_start after their first tool call, which changed the prompt mid-run and broke the provider prompt cache twice per run (#10267, #10739 by @davidbrai)
  • Fixed codemode blocking the process for seconds to minutes after scripts with many text() calls; joining the output items was quadratic (#10678)
  • Fixed print mode (-p) exiting 0 without a warning when the final response was cut off at the model's output token limit; it now prints the partial text, writes a warning to stderr, and exits 1 (#10799)
  • Fixed pi mcp login --help and the CLI docs describing --timeout as only the wait for the browser; it limits the whole sign-in (#10636)
  • Fixed ~ not being expanded in !pattern, +path, and -path entries of the extensions, skills, prompts, and themes settings, so entries like !~/skills/pdf matched nothing (#10765)
  • Fixed MCP servers with resources registering no tools until every resource was listed; Pi no longer lists resources when connecting, so /mcp and pi mcp list show that a server offers resources instead of counting them (#10526)
  • Fixed MCP tool calls failing when the server returns structured content that is not an object (an array, null, or a primitive, as MCP 2026-07-28 allows); codemode declarations of MCP tools without an output schema now type structuredContent as unknown instead of an object (#10416)
  • Fixed reading or writing auth.json on Windows failing with EPERM when the previous lock folder was still being deleted; Pi now retries briefly before reporting the error (#10801)
  • Fixed OpenRouter router models such as openrouter/auto showing a cost of -$1,000,000 per 1M tokens; OpenRouter's negative placeholder prices are now 0 (#10672 by @davidbrai)
  • Fixed Anthropic requests failing when the provider rejects the strict tool set for grammar size, tool count, or union limits; Pi now retries once with preferred tools non-strict (#10409)
  • Fixed "The upstream provider returned an error" not being retried as a transient provider error (#10842)
  • Fixed focused fullscreen overlays blocking mouse-wheel scrolling of the transcript (#10769)
  • Fixed fragmented terminal color replies leaking hex values into the prompt and opening the external editor (related reports: #10256, #10657)
  • Fixed inline images not rendering in Herdr since 1.1.0 (#10774 by @ogulcancelik)
  • Fixed key bindings that name a shifted symbol, such as + or ctrl++, not matching under the Kitty keyboard protocol or modifyOtherKeys (#9880 by @christianklotz)
  • Fixed the compaction status indicator embedded in a custom editor using the default muted colors instead of the editor border or thinking-level color, as the working indicator does (#10809, #10810 by @Jay-Madden)
  • Fixed MCP tool calls that were cancelled or timed out while waiting for an OAuth refresh still being sent to the server once the refresh finished (#10598)
  • Fixed MCP OAuth sign-in accepting an authorization response with an iss parameter that could not be checked because no authorization server metadata was discovered; sign-in now fails with an issuer mismatch. Reported by @AizenvoltPrime
  • Fixed codemode failing with a broken bridge error when Pi runs under node --watch on Node 24 or 26 (#10725)

Removed

  • Removed the unreleased experimental server, client, and plugin stack (src/experimental, src/cli/experimental) and its source-only ./client and ./experimental/plugin exports; none of it was part of the published package. The @earendil-works/pi-client, @earendil-works/pi-protocol, and @earendil-works/pi-server packages it used are removed and no longer released
  • Removed the dependency on @earendil-works/chord

View release →

teng-lin/notebooklm-py ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.8.5 Stable2026-10-12T22:20:11Z

[0.8.5] - 2026-10-12

Added

  • Python 3.15 support (#2480). Add Python 3.15 classifier, documentation, and testing across CI matrices.

Fixed

  • Expired cold-start authentication (#2461, #2464). Exhausted login-redirect recovery raises AuthError, so Python callers can catch it, CLI commands return AUTH_ERROR with exit 1, and MCP tools preserve the re-login hint. Read-only Android auth loading reports the same error without running Web recovery or persisting cookies.

  • Signed-out sessions misreported as a page-structure change (#2479, #2481). The app host now answers a signed-out request with a token-less landing page instead of a sign-in redirect. When the CSRF token is missing, token loading now asks the app's /login endpoint whether the session is signed in. A stale session reports Authentication expired and enters the existing login recovery, instead of CSRF token not found ... page structure has changed.

  • Headless and CDP re-auth no longer capture a signed-out browser session (#2482, #2483). An app-host landing with a SID cookie is no longer enough: the capture first asks the app's /login, through the browser context's own cookies, whether the session is signed in. A signed-out session is refused with HeadlessLoginRequiredError and the saved authentication is kept, instead of being overwritten with another expired set. If the check cannot be answered, capture proceeds as before.

  • Browser session capture validation and auth doctor (#2472). Validate captured browser storage cookies across SPA navigation, refuse anonymous captures, and clarify diagnostic warnings in notebooklm doctor.

  • Tokenless Web auth recovery and generation journal (#2465). Recover tokenless Web authentication and journal generation at submission time.

  • Release archives packaging (#2463). Exclude internal repository assets from release distribution archives.

  • CI resilience and recovery (#2458, #2460, #2462, #2473, #2475). Install impersonate extra for package verification, recover tagged publishes with release-scoped auth allowances, allow scheduler delays in upload and download fault scenarios, and reconcile auth repository lint guardrails.

  • MCP test isolation (#2459). Isolate MCP backend flag tests from ambient user profiles.

Changed

  • FastMCP 4.1.0 and MCP 2.x upgrade (#2480). Bump FastMCP to 4.1.0 and adapt MCP tool annotations and tool result handling for Python 3.15 compatibility.
  • Android protobuf toolchain upgrade (#2480). Upgrade pinned toolchain to grpcio 1.84.0 and protobuf 7.36.2 for Python 3.15 wheel support.
  • Dependency updates. Bump pyjwt to 2.15.0 (#2456), beartype to 0.23.1 (#2480), and librt (#2480).

paperclipai/paperclip ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2026.1012.0 Stable2026-10-12T21:06:53Z

Paperclip v2026.1012.0

Released: 2026-10-12

Paperclip v2026.1012.0 carries 130 commits, promoted from 2026.1006.0-beta.0. The headline is that outside assistants can now join your team: Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, and any MCP client can connect to Paperclip as a signed-in person, review work, and delegate tasks, all behind an experimental setting. Around that: Chat leads the navigation with each agent's tasks and artifacts beside the conversation, the Connections catalog pulls in accounts you already linked in Composio and Arcade, connections carry usage instructions that reach your agents automatically, Claude Sonnet 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol arrive with refreshed harnesses, and agents can safely update company skills. Underneath, Codex runs survive server restarts and model-capacity errors, workspace restore locks move to crash-safe SQLite file locks, and default agent instructions shrink to a fraction of their former size.

Breaking Changes

  • Workspace restore locks change protocol; stop every old process before upgrading. Restore and agent-file collection locks are now OS-backed SQLite file locks under <instance root>/locks/directory-merge/, released by the kernel when a process crashes. Old and new versions must not write to the same instance root at once: drain and stop all old server and worker processes (including other hosts or containers) before upgrading or rolling back, then move any leftover legacy <hash>.lock/ directories to a scratch location. Never delete or move the new .lock.sqlite files. See doc/workspace-restore-locks.md. (#14869)
  • Stored tool grants are enforced as written. A grant whose scope.allow list does not match a tool, connection, or application no longer falls through to a broader policy; grants with unknown selectors or malformed restrictions now deny access. Review custom grants after upgrading if an agent loses a tool it previously used. (#14915)

Highlights

  • Assistants join your team over MCP — A new "Assistant Connection (MCP)" card in Connections lets Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, and generic MCP clients connect to Paperclip through browser OAuth or device-code consent and act as the signed-in user: read and search tasks, create and delegate work, leave human comments, read documents and deliverables, and follow results through signed event webhooks (#14846). Consent shows the client and organization, write access can be opted out, requests can be pinned to one company, and a paperclip mcp CLI device login bridges local stdio clients; it is off by default behind Settings → Experimental → "Assistant connections (MCP)" and requires an HTTPS PAPERCLIP_PUBLIC_URL (#14933).
  • Chat leads the way — With Agent Chat enabled in the streamlined UI (the default shell), Chat is the first row in the Work navigation, the chat rail lists every eligible agent, and the side panel shows that agent's recent tasks and artifacts; a new experimental "Combined Inbox + Task List" setting folds Inbox into Tasks as views (#15100). Instances that switched "Streamlined UI" off keep the legacy navigation and do not see these changes. The Chat entry reopens the last conversation you visited (#14848), and the chat header shows the agent's avatar (#14726).
  • One catalog for every account — The Connections Apps catalog discovers accounts already connected upstream in Composio, Arcade, and supported Executor gateways and groups them with native accounts under one app card, with source chips, search, and pagination (#15254). AgentMail is a default connection with inline setup cards in Agent Chat (#14772), Neon joins the catalog with OAuth or API-key sign-in (#14980), Asana works again with a default "Sign in with Asana" (#14756), and AI accounts gain a "Check usage" action that reads current provider limits for Codex, Claude, Grok, and OpenRouter (#14936).
  • Connections carry instructions for agents — A connection can hold editable usage instructions that are delivered to every authorized agent turn across the native runner, legacy adapters, and HTTP/process contracts. Honcho, Mem0, Zep, Supermemory, and Cognee ship defaults, the editor appears in setup and the Permissions panel, and agent Instructions show a read-only "From connections" section (#15216).
  • New models, refreshed harnesses — Claude Sonnet 5.5 is selectable for Claude Code agents with Low through Max effort, and the qualified Claude runtime moves to Claude Code 2.1.286 (#14993); GPT-6.1 Sol joins the Codex adapter with Fast mode and Ultra effort, Bedrock gains Sonnet 5.5, and the shared pins move to Codex 0.160.0 and OpenCode 1.18.34 (#14942). Claude and Codex model menus now list models in the same order as the Claude and ChatGPT apps (#14917, #14918).
  • Agents maintain company skills — Native-runner agents get an update_skill tool that rewrites a company skill with version checking and safe retries, recorded in version history and the audit log; the skill file API gains optional expectedVersionId and idempotencyKey fields (#15049). Newly created agents receive a default set of configuration grants so they can finish agent, tool, skill, and task setup without manual permission work (#15283).
  • Codex runs that survive restarts and capacity limits — A native Codex turn killed by a server restart resumes in the same conversation once the old process is proven gone (#15297); a model at capacity shows a "Model at capacity" card and retries automatically after one and then two minutes (#15347); a model the ChatGPT account cannot use shows "Model unavailable" with guidance instead of generic recovery (#15299, #15304); and Codex keeps its stock base prompt, receiving Paperclip's context as additive developer instructions (#14920).
  • A rebuilt New task dialog — The dialog is built on the shared task composer with a Project and Worktrees bar, remembered assignee, project, and effort, and task mentions in suggestions; the status, reviewer, approver, and watchdog controls move out of the dialog into task properties, and new tasks default to the last assignee used (#15091). Tasks created without a title get a clean plain-text provisional title (#15047).
  • Far shorter default instructions — New companies and hires get role descriptions instead of procedural manuals: the default CEO instructions drop from roughly 1,900 words to 20 (#14985), new non-CEO hires receive a one-sentence default manual (#14948), and the bundled planning skills shrink to about 400 words combined and no longer force a child-task matrix (#15296). Existing saved manuals and custom bundles are unchanged.

Fixes

  • Native runner and sessions — A run whose final answer was saved but whose shutdown failed reaches its correct final status, and the agent list_projects tool returns paged summaries instead of overflowing the command limit (#15217); tasks stopped during native preparation on an older build regain Retry and accept new messages (#15020, #15022); a continuation cancelled by the queued-run review gate is treated as a review wait instead of an unknown-execution hold (#15024); Retry and saved-message recovery work on SQL_ASCII databases (#15033); a user-continued legacy run that times out can be retried automatically (#14987); a completed run no longer reports "Run failed" when instruction-file cleanup times out afterward (#14945) and that cleanup no longer blocks lease release (#14866); write_document and other semantic tool calls no longer fail when a document merely discusses credentials (#14937) or contains credential-looking text (#15301); long connected MCP tool names are aliased to fit provider limits and the recovery notice explains why a run was cancelled with a Continue option (#14951); agents keep their task context when a tool connection changes mid-task (#14963); native MCP gateways reject run tokens from any agent other than their owner (#14914); and the Codex runner's "ignored unrelated provider information" warnings stay in the run log instead of the chat (#14929).
  • Sandboxes and workspaces — Sandbox runs no longer fail with workspace_restore_failed after an agent rebases or amends its branch (#15268); the Daytona driver recovers output from a log socket that goes silent (#14889); bridge control commands are capped at 30 seconds and active runs are flagged as suspicious after 5 minutes of silence and critical after 15 (#14979); multi-repository projects round-trip commits on SSH execution environments (#14782); isolated-worktree tasks on the Paperclip repository itself set up without a local seed config (#14810); and the standard Docker image bundles the remote provider pack so remote OpenCode and ACPX runs no longer fail with runner_remote_provider_artifact_incompatible (#14854).
  • Chat and task UI — Steer and Interrupt show your message immediately and deliver to the active run even after a model or effort change, and a stopped native startup offers Retry (#15015); a message sent after a failed native run no longer hangs and is delivered exactly once (#14857); stale "Run failed", "Stopped", and "Waiting to resume" markers disappear once a later attempt supersedes them (#14874); dismissed agent questions stay in the task feed and remain answerable (#15229); confirmation cards show "Preparing approval…" until the source workspace is ready instead of erroring on click (#14893); an unsaved Agent Chat no longer polls the browser panel with a draft id every three seconds (#15298); the thread no longer calls a stale send or cancel action (#14863); mobile task pages get an opaque footer, steadier scrolling, a full-height document panel, and in-place document links (#15228) with new output staying above the composer (#15282); the completed-work caret sits beside its label (#15048), composer labels use the available width (#15050), branch artifacts without a remote link say so (#15051), the Dashboard shows each task once (#14847), and a selected saved subscription can be used without clicking the provider tile (#14996).
  • Connections and access — Agent settings can reconnect an unavailable personal AI account in place, and a newly connected account becomes the user's default (#14916); generic MCP OAuth requests offline_access when offered so access survives token expiry (#14950); MCP tool discovery uses a fraction of the memory and queries on large catalogs, bounds concurrent listings with a tool_discovery_busy 503 under load, and answers GET on stateless endpoints with 405 (#14864); low-trust agents can create self-assigned work inside their permitted scope and update their own managed AGENTS.md from the owner's chat (#14870) and accept their owner's direct messages and human-assigned tasks outside an intake boundary (#14775); company skill imports from a server-managed project checkout no longer fail with skill_workspace_boundary_denied (#10329); and the managed git/gh launcher retries a failed credential broker request before continuing without managed credentials (#14856).
  • Server resilience — Dashboard reads (#14873), scoped read queries (#15248), and the task run list (#15300) retry transient PostgreSQL disconnects instead of failing the request, and routine routes return 404 for malformed ids instead of a database error (#15313).

Improvements

  • Experimental AI connection pools rotate new tasks across saved AI accounts while each task stays pinned to its account, behind the default-off enableAiConnectionRouters setting and a router plugin (#14967); the exe.dev sandbox provider can clone a prepared "Source VM" instead of creating a bare one (#14975); the native runner daemon accepts any --listen-port so several listening runners can share a host (#15353); the server keeps a durable lifecycle journal of agent hire, pause, resume, and terminate events and project create and update events as groundwork for plugin hooks (#15280, #15306); Slack, GitHub, MCP, invitation, routine, and handoff prompts share one animated "copy prompt" control (#14862) and the Slack setup prompt matches the current flow (#14965); the per-action Test dialog renders MCP results as readable tables and cards (#14859); agents can create question cards from a single canonical question set (#14946) and receive identical completion-tool guidance across Codex, Claude, and OpenCode (#14961) with less repeated procedure text and a canonical link from write_document (#15151); and failed restores and Stop timeouts record which stage failed and why in run results and Sentry context (#15005, #15291, #14990, #14986).

Upgrade Guide

  • Migrations 0294–0305 run on upgrade: 0294 adds a tool_mcp_gateway_tokens expiry index; 0295 and 0296 add aggregator account snapshot and sync tables; 0297 adds resource_lifecycle_events and 0300 rewrites its action check constraint; 0298 adds tool_connections.agent_instructions and backfills default instructions for existing Honcho, Mem0, Zep, Supermemory, and Cognee connections; 0299 adds AI connection pool, router cursor, and task pin tables plus company_secrets.ai_session_epoch; 0301–0305 add the public MCP OAuth client, grant, token, receipt, event subscription, device-flow, and admission tables. All are additive; 0298 is the only one that updates existing rows.
  • Stop all old processes before upgrading (#14869): the new SQLite restore-lock protocol cannot coexist with the old directory locks. Drain every server and worker that writes to the instance root, move leftover legacy <hash>.lock/ directories out of <instance root>/locks/directory-merge/, then start the new version. The same applies to rollback.
  • Review custom tool grants (#14915): grants with unknown scope selectors or malformed restrictions now deny instead of falling through.
  • New experimental settings, all off by default: "Assistant connections (MCP)" (enablePublicMcp) exposes the public MCP endpoint and requires PAPERCLIP_PUBLIC_URL to be an HTTPS origin (#14933); "Combined Inbox + Task List" (enableCombinedInboxTasks) folds Inbox into Tasks and takes effect only while "Streamlined UI" is on (#15100); enableAiConnectionRouters turns on connection pools and has no UI toggle (#14967).
  • Connection instructions for custom adapters (#15216): HTTP adapters receive a top-level connectionInstructions field in the request body, either a { text, digest } object or null when no instructions apply; process adapters receive PAPERCLIP_CONNECTION_INSTRUCTIONS_FILE, the path to a private JSON file holding the same { text, digest } object, and the variable is set to an empty string (not a path) when no instructions apply. Adapters should append the object's text to the model prompt rather than passing the object through. Built-in harnesses need no change.
  • Harness pins moved to Codex 0.160.0, Claude Agent SDK 0.3.286 (Claude Code 2.1.286), and OpenCode 1.18.34 (#14942, #14993). Custom sandbox images built with Claude Code older than 2.1.284 must be rebuilt before Sonnet 5.5 works on the CLI path, and the controller and runner should be deployed before promoting a sandbox image with the new pins; remote Codex now accepts 0.149.0 up to but excluding 0.161.0.
  • New defaults: newly created standard agents receive default configuration grants, while existing agents are unchanged (#15283); the runner's --listen-port still defaults to 43127 (#15353); silent active runs are flagged after 5 and 15 minutes instead of 1 and 4 hours, with no automatic cancellation (#14979).
  • No other configuration or API changes require action.

Contributors

This release has 130 commits from 9 contributors. Thank you to everyone who contributed to this release!

@baozdemir, @valentinpalkovic, @abderbejaoui

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.10.3 Stable2026-10-12T19:23:53Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Breaking Changes

  • Removed estimateImageContentTokens() from @oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core/image-tokens; use Tokenizer.imageTokens(size, detail), which prices with the reading model's catalog image rule (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • applyShakeRegion() and applyShakeRegions() take the replacement's token count (replacementTokens); a block rewrite of an assistant turn adds the tokens it removes to elidedOutputTokens, so a replayed Claude turn's count stays lowered after a reload (#15446 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed Claude context counts reading Opus 5.5 requests about 55% over their billed input: a replayed thinking turn now counts at the output size Claude billed for it, not its signature's bytes (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed screenshots and other non-frame images being counted at the OpenAI rate for every model instead of the reading model's own; Claude billed them about 16% higher (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed OpenAI Responses and Codex context estimates leaving out replayed reasoning: each turn whose encrypted reasoning actually replays now counts its reported reasoning tokens (its ciphertext when the backend reports none), and none while a cold session rebuilds it out of the request, so compaction and snapcompact sizing match billing; branch summaries no longer budget for encrypted reasoning they never send (#15447 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Breaking Changes

  • isOfficialCodexApiUrl moved from @oh-my-pi/pi-ai to @oh-my-pi/pi-catalog/wire/codex, next to CODEX_BASE_URL, so the catalog's compat resolver classifies Codex endpoints with the same check (#15434 by @will-bogusz)

Added

  • Added withoutUnverifiedThinking utility to filter reasoning capability from models when no effort is requested
  • Added reachesOfficialEndpoint(model): whether a request to the model reaches the official endpoint its compat was built for, following ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL and Foundry's FOUNDRY_BASE_URL reroutes of first-party Anthropic models (#15434 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added AssistantMessage.elidedOutputTokens, the visible output tokens a history rewrite (shake) has removed from the turn, which usage.output still counts (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • Exported shouldDropTruncatedThinkingOnlyAssistant from providers/transform-messages, isColdReplayableResponsesTurn from providers/openai-shared and the new openAIResponsesReplaysNativeHistory from providers/openai-responses, so local token estimates can follow which turns replay drops and whether a session replays native history (#15447 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed Claude on Google Antigravity failing every turn with "image exceeds 5 MB maximum" once a large image entered the conversation; images are now downscaled to the host's limits before sending, as on the Anthropic API (#15444 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Breaking Changes

  • resolveInlineImageByteBudget(model, officialEndpoint) and resolveImageCountBudget(model, officialEndpoint) take whether the request reaches the official endpoint instead of reading compat.officialEndpoint, which misses request-time reroutes such as ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL; pass pi-ai's reachesOfficialEndpoint(model) (#15434 by @will-bogusz)

Added

  • Added Muse Wonder (muse-wonder-1.0) to Meta and Muse Code with its minimal–max effort ladder and pricing
  • Added the thinking-unverified rule axis: on hosts whose model list carries no capability metadata (Meta, Muse Code), unrecognized models now offer an opt-in thinking ladder (ThinkingConfig.unverified) instead of none
  • Added thinking-unverified axis for offering reasoning ladders on unclassified model IDs
  • Added Muse Wonder 1.0 model definition to Meta provider
  • Added the opt-in snapcompact-image-reads axis: snapcompact images a fresh read result only for readers whose rule sets it; Claude Opus 5.5 and later opt in (#14249 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added the image-count-budget rule for how many images one request may carry (Anthropic 90, Codex 200, other hosts unchanged) and the official-image-count-budget override that applies only on the official endpoint: Anthropic 600 (100 on 200k-window Claude models), Codex 2,000 (#15434 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added a 24 MB inline-image byte budget for the Codex backend, which now counts as its official endpoint at its default URL and at paths under it such as /codex/responses (#15434 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added isOfficialCodexApiUrl to wire/codex, moved from pi-ai (#15434 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added verified to image-tokenization rules, marking the Claude 4.7+ high-res tier and the GPT-5.5 to 6.1 and gpt-6-astra rules as matching measured provider billing (#15442 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added the image-max-edge axis and resolveImageMaxEdge() for hosts that shrink images before the model reads them; Cursor's Claude and GPT models and its default (Auto) route fit 1024×1024 (#15448 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added the thinking-replay-tokens axis; Claude lines set it to output on every host, since a replayed signed thinking block bills the turn's full reasoning, not the signature's bytes (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added a KDL opt-in for sibling subagent prompt-cache sharing, enabled for Muse Code's Spark 1.3, Wonder 1.0, and Wonder Full 1.0 variants over the Responses API (#15467 by @iliaal)

Changed

  • Updated model definitions and compatibility rules for provider metadata
  • Google Antigravity, xAI and Cursor requests keep up to 60 images, 20 for Claude on Antigravity and Cursor, up from 5; this counts snapcompact frames and live screenshots together (#15449 by @will-bogusz)
  • Snapcompact sizes archives at the frame prices measured for Antigravity Gemini (a fixed 1,089 tokens), Grok 4.6 to 4.20, and Claude Sonnet 4.6's standard tier (#15449 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Applied max-image-dimension and max-image-payload-bytes to Google API models, and declared the 5 MiB per-image limit of Claude on Google Antigravity (#15444 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed local token counts for OpenAI GPT-5 and later models (Codex, OpenAI, Azure, OpenRouter, Copilot and other hosts) reading about 12% low: their text is now counted with o200k_base instead of a bytes/4 estimate (#15447 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Added task.agentSoftRequestBudget to give named subagents their own soft request budget, so a long-running agent can get more requests (or 0 for no guard) while scout, sonic and other agents keep theirs (#15419 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added o200k as a tokenizer value for custom models and overrides in models.yml (#15447 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Sized snapcompact archives for Claude 4.7+ and GPT-5.5 to 6.1 (gpt-6-astra included) at each frame's measured price instead of at least 5,024 tokens, so Codex archives can hold up to 1.7× as many frames when the room under the compaction trigger is the limit (#15442 by @will-bogusz)
  • Enabled sibling subagent prompt-cache sharing for Muse Code's Spark 1.3, Wonder 1.0, and Wonder Full 1.0 variants while keeping their conversations separate (#15467 by @iliaal, based on #15285 by @YueyuHoshizora)
  • Reduced tool-result tokens by omitting subsecond bash timing and redundant task size metadata (#15468 by @iliaal; #15265; based on #15284 by @YueyuHoshizora)
  • On the official Anthropic and Codex endpoints, snapcompact archives may carry up to 12 MB of frames instead of 3 MB, Anthropic requests keep up to 600 images (100 on 200k-window models) instead of 90, and Codex requests up to 2,000 instead of 200; custom base URLs, including Anthropic requests rerouted by ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL or Foundry's FOUNDRY_BASE_URL, keep the previous limits and no longer get Anthropic's 24 MB inline image byte limit (#15434 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed snapcompact.toolResults re-sending each large tool result as images a turn after it went out as text, which broke the prompt cache and Anthropic signed thinking every turn; results are now imaged from their first send, and read results stay text unless the model is known to edit exactly from images (Opus 5.5+) (#14249 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed compaction on Claude sessions with thinking sometimes keeping about half the recent history compaction.keepRecentTokens asks for, because each kept turn was counted at its signature's size (#15446 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed retry fallback rejecting a model with a smaller window by charging it the reasoning the active model's provider usage replayed, and the output cap charging reasoning a cold Responses session leaves out of its first request (#15447 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed anonymized session exports losing whether bash timing was shown (#15468 by @iliaal)
  • Added omp --restore <session>: resumes a session like --resume, then goes on with what its last process left unfinished when it crashed or was killed, both the interrupted turn and the subagents it was running (their results reach the parent as usual); Tern's session daemon now restarts omp with it after a crash
  • Kept private prompt-cache routing keys out of shared sessions and HTML exports (#15467 by @iliaal)

@oh-my-pi/snapcompact

Breaking Changes

  • providerImageBudget(provider) and providerFrameBudget(provider) became providerImageBudget(model) and providerFrameBudget(model), and maxFramesForDataBudget(shape) became maxFramesForDataBudget(shape, budget) with a required byte budget (#15434 by @will-bogusz)
  • Removed PROVIDER_IMAGE_BUDGETS and FRAME_DATA_BYTES_BUDGET: image counts come from the catalog's image-count-budget rules, and frame byte budgets from frameDataBytesBudget(model) (DEFAULT_FRAME_DATA_BYTES_BUDGET where the host has none) (#15434 by @will-bogusz)

Added

  • Added plannedFrameTokens and Shape.frameTokensVerified: archive sizing charges a frame its own price when the reader's image-tokenization rule is verified, and at least FRAME_TOKEN_ESTIMATE otherwise (#15442 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed snapcompact frames for Claude and GPT on Cursor, including its default (Auto) route, being drawn at 1568–1932px, which Cursor shrank to 1024px before the model read them; frames now render at the host's catalog image-max-edge (#15448 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Changed

  • Updated model picker to offer manual effort selection for unverified models instead of automatic mode

Fixed

  • Fixed the snapcompact.shape settings preview labelling variants 5x8-sent and repeating the first variant's sample when the frame edge differs from the variant table (Claude and GPT on Cursor; auto on high-resolution Claude) (#15448 by @will-bogusz)

What's Changed

  • feat(task): let named subagents get their own soft request budget by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15419
  • feat(catalog,snapcompact,coding-agent): size snapcompact request limits from catalog policy by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15434
  • fix(catalog,snapcompact,coding-agent): size snapcompact archives at billing-verified frame prices by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15442
  • fix(ai,catalog): clamp Claude images to Antigravity's 5 MiB limit by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15444
  • fix(catalog,snapcompact,tui): keep snapcompact frames legible to Claude and GPT on Cursor by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15448
  • feat(catalog): size snapcompact archives on Antigravity, xAI and Cursor from measured limits and prices by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15449
  • fix(agent,catalog,coding-agent): count replayed Claude thinking and images at what Claude bills by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15446
  • perf(tools): trim bash timing and task size metadata by @iliaal in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/15468

sinelaw/fresh ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.5.3fresh-editor 0.5.3 Stable2026-10-12T19:12:22Z

0.5.3

For live updates on Fresh, follow me on X.

Most config options below can be changed in the Settings UI - run Open Settings from the command palette (Ctrl+P).

Features

  • Find Next selects the whole match - F3 / Shift+F3 select the entire match, and the current match is highlighted in its own color
  • Theme text attributes - any theme color accepts bold, italic or underline, e.g. to draw diagnostics as underlines (#3494, requested by @zeevro)
  • Theme color fallbacks - colors a theme omits are derived from its other colors. Complete user themes may look slightly different after upgrading; see Themes → Fallbacks
  • Full internationalization of the Settings UI - setting names, descriptions and categories are translated in all locales (#3479 by @neatsorg)
  • Import coding agent and terminal sessions in Orchestrator
    • Claude and Codex sessions running outside Fresh are listed in the dock, grouped by product
    • Open a session as a workspace, or fork a cloud session into one
    • Move to Folder files workspaces and sessions into folders, by click or drag
  • File Explorer (#3427, requested by @akarinotomoshibi)
    • Drag and drop to move files and folders
    • Rename accepts a path, which moves the entry
    • Dropping a file asks for confirmation before moving it, and the move is animated
    • Refresh button
  • Weather location and units can be set for the dashboard plugin (#3344 by @KyleKorndoerfer)
  • Kotlin uses JetBrains' official kotlin-lsp by default; set lsp.kotlin.command to keep kotlin-language-server (#3481 by @karczews)
  • Plugin docs - the API reference is generated from the source, and there is a new "Asking for Input" guide

Bug Fixes

  • AutoOpenDock honored, with auto / always / never values - opening or closing the dock is remembered across restarts, and the old on/off value is migrated (#3442, #3516, reported by @sjsepan3)
  • Bare fresh on a Linux console and with several terminals attached (#3517, reported by @MAT-services)
    • Faster startup: the first frame no longer waits for plugins to load
    • The session is sized by the terminal last used
    • Console mouse (GPM) works again; selections and indentation guides are visible
    • The dock width scales with the terminal instead of crowding the menu bar
  • Line Numbers, Line Wrap and Current Line Highlight apply to all open documents, and the View menu reflects the current state (#3449, reported by @sjsepan3)
  • File Explorer (#3427, reported by @akarinotomoshibi)
    • New File / New Folder create the entry only after the name is confirmed
    • Rename no longer overwrites an existing file
    • Right-click uses the entry under the pointer: empty space selects the project root, and each segment of a dir1/dir2/dir3 row is selectable
  • Project Search/Replace - $1 capture groups are expanded in regex mode, hidden files are searched in current-file scope, and Replace All asks for confirmation in a dialog
  • Selection keys - Up/Down work when a selection touches the first or last line (#3486, reported by @Korkman); repeated Ctrl+L extends the selection by a line (#3495, reported by @sjsepan3)
  • Plugin timers and crash-recovery saves run in the background session (#3440, reported by @neatsorg)
  • Restored unnamed buffers use the configured editor settings, e.g. current line highlight (#3426, reported by @neatsorg)
  • LSP diagnostics are shown on the only line of an empty file (#3484, reported by @akarinotomoshibi)
  • Mouse release no longer printed at the shell prompt after quitting with a click (#3474, reported by @odjbo)
  • Update check on Android/Termux no longer panics (#3453, reported by @dsvarela)
  • Unsaved buffers are kept when switching projects - they are restored in the project they belong to and no longer multiply across workspace switches and restarts (#3475, #3476, reported by @Korkman; #3530 by @neatsorg)
  • Scroll position is kept when a modifier key is pressed alone (#3324, reported by @akarinotomoshibi; #3373 by @56steve)
  • Quit is no longer delayed by a background request or a lost mouse release
  • A second daemon can no longer take over a running session and leave it unreachable
  • TypeScript 7 (tsc --lsp) diagnostics work (#3061, reported by @defaultkavy; #3413 by @atk)
  • Window title is set in Orchestrator sessions (#3515 by @neatsorg)
  • Dev container remote indicator is cleared once the container is attached (#3487 by @cychitivav)
  • Occurrence highlight skips the indentation of a wrapped line (#3466, reported by @mandolyte)
  • Review mode asks for confirmation before discarding a line selection
  • Syntax highlighting
    • Nim: an unpaired apostrophe no longer colors the rest of the file, and 'suffix number literals are highlighted (#3455, reported by @codic12)
    • JavaScript/TypeScript: a < comparison no longer starts JSX highlighting (#3527)
  • Empty pane is dimmed behind Settings (#3452, reported by @akarinotomoshibi)
  • List selection follows its item when the list is re-sorted or filtered, and Page Up/Down move by the visible rows
  • Word wrap of long CJK text no longer drops the end of a line (#3438 by @neatsorg)
  • Review Diff no longer draws the scrollbar of the hidden file behind it (#3434)
  • Windows GUI build from cmd.exe and PowerShell - a new fresh-gui-<target>.zip ships fresh-gui.exe with a fresh-gui.com console launcher, so typing fresh-gui runs the TUI without the shell competing for keyboard and mouse input (#2965, reported by @mokurin000)

Internals

  • Plugin API breaking change - setPromptSuggestions throws unless every suggestion has a unique id (new, required); showActionPopup / setLspMenuContributions throw on duplicate ids; widget List / Tree specs whose itemKeys don't match their items one to one are refused
  • Flaky e2e test fixes and internal cleanups

View release →

supermemoryai/supermemory ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
server-v0.0.9Supermemory Local 0.0.9 Stable2026-10-12T18:59:36Z
  • Added prebuilt Docker images for Linux amd64 and arm64.
  • Improved Local uploads, namespace operations, search scoping and CLI commands.
  • Added API v5 support. See the API v5 upgrade guide.

Before upgrading, back up your store and keep your existing directory and API key. Localhost requests now require the key; WORKFLOW_ENGINE=direct is no longer supported.

Before migrating an existing store, Local verifies a complete original backup. Existing cleanup can remove older duplicate documents and their source links.

Native Windows remains on 0.0.8 for now.

OpenWhispr/openwhispr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.10.31.10.3 Stable2026-10-12T16:56:16Z

OpenWhispr 1.10.3

The assistant can now act in other apps: it drafts and sends email, posts to Slack, and works with Linear and GitHub issues, always behind a card you approve. Notes get templates for the AI Summary, actions that answer in a docked chat, and real tables. OpenWhispr also uses far less memory when idle, shows a plain-language error when your API key or provider fails, and fixes a long list of bugs on Linux, Windows and macOS.

🌟 Highlights

  • 🔌 Connectors — The assistant can draft and send email (Gmail), post to Slack, and work with Linear and GitHub issues. Nothing goes out until you press Send on a card showing exactly what will be sent. Paid plans; connect in Settings → Integrations. (#2331, #2369, #2385, #2401, #2402)
  • 📝 Note templates and actions — Templates shape the AI Summary, and actions like Follow-up email answer in a chat docked beside the note instead of overwriting your summary. (#2397, #2557, #2578, #2598)
  • 📊 Tables in notes — Tables stay tables when you edit a note, and a formatting toolbar appears when you select text. (#2255)
  • 🪶 Lighter when idle — Search and meeting detection only run when needed, cutting idle memory use from about 500 MB. (#2299, #2371)
  • 💬 Clear error messages — When your own API key or provider fails, you get a plain-language message with a link to fix it. (#2510)

✨ Also new

Echo cancellation for meetings, an option to keep the local model loaded, a folder picker in the meeting notification, dictation audio that survives a crash, a macOS permission helper in onboarding, Apple sign-in on Windows and Linux, and live speaker names on Linux.

🛠 Fixes

  • Windows: Teams meetings record everyone for the whole call again. (#2233)
  • Transcription: API key changes apply without a restart, and AssemblyAI, Corti and Gemini Live no longer drop words.
  • Assistant: Answers paste as plain text, follow-ups don't repeat actions already sent, and images in replies are no longer loaded automatically.
  • Linux: GPU rendering is back (except on NVIDIA), Settings no longer lags, and paste waits for you to release held keys.
  • Notes and meetings: Assistant edits to a note can be undone and never wipe fields by accident, clearing a note now syncs, and fixes for Join & transcribe, live transcripts on reload, and TXT export. (#2602, #2604)

⚠️ Heads-up

The Windows portable .exe is discontinued. Use the installer instead. (#2301)

The full list is in the changelog.

🙏 Thanks

Thanks to @Chadpiha, @boseq, @greatcoat, @hsusul, @IdrisGit, @clarkmaan, @Nathan-Roll1, @gabrielste1n, @sochotnicky, @kuleje, @TrackerGit, @zhongwater123, @emanuelet, @marselsel, @folks-samuel-cb, @STiFLeR7, @Adityakk9031, @stantheman0128, @cadufpolis, @Abhishek-B-R, @dajiaohuang and @hiro-nikaitou for their work on this release. 🎉

And thanks to @jaszczurovsky, @TaiFeng, @jsholcomb, @wadamek65, @KishenG and @amarpreet2209 for reporting and narrowing down the Teams capture bug (#1265).

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.10CC Switch v4.0.10 Stable2026-10-12T16:38:21Z

CC Switch v4.0.10

4.0 正式版的第六个修复版本

这一版的重点是一键导入链接（ccswitch:// 深度链接）：CC Switch 没在运行时点链接不会再丢；链接可以声明接口格式、导入 Pi 供应商、带上网站自己的图标；Codex 和 Grok Build 的链接保留供应商自带的配置，不再被换成固定模板；确认框里可以选导入后是否立即切换。同时发布了中、英、日三语的站长接入指南和在线链接生成器。供应商图标现在可以上传本地图片，存进「我的图标」后所有供应商都能选用；用量页新增首字时间，没开路由也能按会话日志估算。另外修复了路由模式下 Claude Code 的 haiku 档请求被要求思考的上游拒绝、v3.20 及更早用官方账号走路由留下的 Codex 会话打不开、Grok Build 第三方供应商生成不了会话标题等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

一键导入链接

用户能感受到的变化

  • CC Switch 没在运行时点链接，不会再丢。 以前在 Windows 和 Linux 上，CC Switch 没开着时点一键导入链接，应用会启动，但导入确认框不出现；轻量模式下窗口重建时也会丢。macOS 上同一个链接会弹两次确认框。现在链接会先存下来，确认框显示出来之后才算处理完，中途被打断也不会丢，后到的旧链接也不会盖掉新链接。
  • 确认框里可以选导入后是否立即切换。 供应商链接的确认框新增「导入后立即切换到这个供应商」开关，默认关闭，打开时会提示客户端请求将改发到链接里的地址。OpenCode、OpenClaw、Hermes、MiniMax Code 这类共存式应用是「导入后立即添加到 OpenCode 配置」这样的开关，Pi 是「导入后立即在 Pi 中启用」，这两类默认打开；提示词是「导入后立即启用」。链接里的 enabled 只决定开关的初始状态。关掉开关就只保存供应商，不改客户端配置，即使这是该应用的第一个供应商。
  • 带用量查询脚本的链接，导入后默认开启用量查询。 站长在链接里附上脚本，就是想让你导入后直接看到余额，以前还要再手动打开一次。链接写了 usageEnabled=false 时仍然关闭；不带脚本的链接和以前一样。脚本在沙箱里运行，只能向供应商地址的同源 HTTPS 发一次请求，确认框里照常完整显示脚本内容。
  • Codex 链接保留供应商自带的配置。 链接带完整 config.toml 时，以前只取出 Key、地址和模型，再套进固定模板，供应商的认证方式、请求头、WebSocket 支持和模型目录地址都会丢。现在保留其中属于这个供应商的部分：连接和认证字段、模型、推理强度、上下文窗口、子代理模型等；模型目录地址只在和接口地址同源时保留，因为 Codex 拉取它时会带上供应商的凭据。[features]、MCP、权限这类全局设置不导入。会让 Codex 执行命令或读取本机凭据的供应商配置（auth、aws、gateway_oauth、env_http_headers）整条拒绝。不带配置的链接导入结果和以前逐字一致。
  • Grok Build 链接保留模型配置。 带 config.toml 的链接以前也会被换成固定模板，丢掉显示名、说明、接口类型和上下文窗口。现在保留 [models] default 指向的那张模型表，地址、Key 和模型仍以链接参数为准；用到 auth_provider、env_http_headers、mtls_cert_dir 的链接会被拒绝。一个供应商在 Grok Build 里只占一张表，所以其他模型表不导入。
  • 会被展开执行的值一律拒绝。 共存式应用会展开配置里的一些特殊写法：Pi 里以 ! 开头的值会被当成命令执行，$NAME / ${NAME} 会读环境变量；OpenCode 有 {env:} / {file:}；OpenClaw 和 Hermes 有 ${...}。链接里出现这些写法时，可能执行命令，或者把本机的密钥和文件内容发到链接里的地址。现在解析链接、合并配置和生成供应商时都会检查，发现就拒绝导入。
  • 其他： 带 configUrl 的链接现在一打开就提示不支持，不会等你确认后才失败；提示词链接支持 MiniMax Code，MCP 链接支持 Pi 和 MiniMax Code；导入供应商后托盘菜单会立即刷新。

站长能用的新参数

  • apiFormat： 一个参数声明上游的接口格式（anthropic / openai_chat / openai_responses / gemini_native），CC Switch 会换成各应用自己的字段。Claude Code、Codex、Grok Build 的非原生格式需要开启本地路由；应用不支持的组合在解析时就会被拒绝。不带这个参数的链接行为不变。
  • Pi 供应商链接： 新增支持，导入后写进 Pi 的 models.json，链接必须带模型。
  • iconUrl： 填网站自己 logo 的 https 地址。确认框打开时就下载并显示在顶部，导入后作为卡片的自定义图标；下载失败不影响导入，卡片退回内置图标或名称首字母。图片会重绘成小尺寸 PNG 保存，地址本身不保存也不渲染。下载时只连公网地址，并锁定解析结果，防止第二次 DNS 解析指向别处；重定向逐跳检查，最多 3 跳。不走系统代理、不带 Cookie，5 秒超时，最大 256 KB，文件类型必须和内容一致。

站长接入指南和在线生成器

  • 站长接入指南（另有 English、日本語）：怎么生成链接、怎么在页面上放「一键导入」按钮（点按钮时再向后端取链接，Key 不会出现在页面源码里）、用户没装 CC Switch 时显示什么、怎么附上用量查询脚本。用 New API 或 Sub2API 搭的站，Key 列表里已经自带「导入到 CC Switch」按钮；区别是 New API 的链接不带用量查询脚本，用户导入后看不到余额。
  • 在线链接生成器：从测试页改成给站长用的生成器，分供应商、MCP、提示词、Skill 四类，按应用列出可选的 apiFormat，内置用量脚本模板，填 icon / iconUrl 时按应用内同样的规则检查。用户手册里原来的生成器链接指向一个从没开通的 GitHub Pages 地址，打开是 404，现在手册和 README 都改指这里。

新增

供应商图标可以上传本地图片，存进「我的图标」反复使用。 图标选择器分成「我的图标」和「内置图标」两组，搜索框同时筛两组。点「我的图标」第一格的「上传」，选一张 PNG、JPG、WebP 或 SVG（不超过 2 MB），起个名字（默认是文件名）就存进「我的图标」并用在当前供应商上；以后其他供应商、其他应用都能直接选，不用再传一次，重复上传同一张图会复用已有的那条。鼠标移到图标上可以重命名或删除；卡片保存的是图片本身，所以重命名、删除都不影响已经在用它的供应商。图片会缩成 96×96 的 PNG，「我的图标」和供应商配置都随云同步带走，不在磁盘上另存文件；SVG 会先转成位图，里面的脚本和外部请求都不会执行，原始文件不进数据库。一键导入链接带来的网站图标会以「当前图标」显示，可以存进「我的图标」。选择器现在点一下图标就选好并关闭，去掉图标点搜索框旁的「清除图标」。

用量页显示首字时间。 路由服务一直记录首字时间，以前只在悬停提示和请求详情里能看到。现在供应商统计和模型统计新增「首字」列，取经路由服务的成功流式请求的中位数，偶尔排队、冷启动的慢请求不会把它拉高；请求日志在「速度」后面也加了「首字」列。没开路由时，按会话日志的时间戳估算，带 ≈ 显示：Claude Code 看以思考开头的回复，Codex 看较新版本写入的时间戳；以正文或工具调用开头的回复没有估算值。升级前导入的记录不补算。

用量查询新增 Sub2API 模板。 「配置用量查询」的模板里新增 Sub2API，只需要供应商的 Key 和地址，地址带不带 /v1 都行；会显示套餐名，额度不限的订阅不再显示成负余额。

新增预设： NekoCode（Claude Code、Claude Desktop、Codex、OpenCode、OpenClaw、Hermes、Pi，MiniMax Code 沿用 Pi 预设）。YYLX 在中文界面显示为「鱼鱼连线」（繁体中文为「魚魚連線」）。

修复

  • 路由模式下 Claude Code 的 haiku 档请求被上游拒绝（#8118）。路由模式往 settings.json 里写的是固定的模型别名，Claude Code 按别名决定怎么发思考参数。haiku 档以前写的是 claude-haiku-4-5，Claude Code 认为这个模型可以关思考，就发出关闭思考的请求，要求必须开思考的上游会直接拒绝。现在改成 claude-haiku-5-5，和 sonnet、opus 档的发法一致。路由本身仍按「haiku」关键词映射，选到哪个模型不变。
  • v3.20 及更早用官方账号走路由留下的 Codex 会话打不开（#7948）。v3.17 到 v3.20 用官方账号走本地路由时，Codex 把会话记在 cc-switch-official 名下；v4.0 不再写这张表，Codex 恢复这些会话时就会报「Model provider cc-switch-official not found」。现在 CC Switch 启动时会把它们移回 Codex 内置的 openai 分组，只改会话记录，不碰 config.toml。这些会话本来就用官方登录，Codex 也一直把它们当 OpenAI 处理，移动后行为不变。
  • Grok Build 用第三方供应商时生成不了会话标题（#7003）。没设 models.session_summary 时，Grok Build 用内置的 grok-4.6 生成标题，却把请求发到默认模型表的地址，第三方上游会拒绝。现在切换供应商时，session_summary 在没设或指向 CC Switch 写的表时会跟着指向当前供应商，切回官方账号时删掉；你自己设的内置模型或自己的表保持不动。
  • OpenRouter、Novita 余额为零时卡片显示「套餐已过期」（#8148）。余额为零或为负时，Key 其实还能用，卡片和托盘却显示套餐已过期。现在显示「余额已用完」；接口返回缺失或格式不对的余额时报错，不再当成零余额。
  • Codex 中途换模型后，用量记在了原来的模型上。 Codex 在线程设置里换模型时，日志里不一定会写新的轮次上下文，用量导入以前会把之后的请求记在上一个模型名下（没有更早记录时记成 unknown），价格也按原模型算。现在会读取这条设置。已经导入的记录不会自动改写，源会话日志还在的话，可以在用量页的「数据来源」里点「重建 Codex 用量」（重建前会自动备份数据库）。
  • 在设置里改了配置目录、还没点保存，改另一项设置时目录也被保存了（#7849）。其他设置自动保存时，现在只会带上已确认的目录；被放弃的草稿也不会再留在输入框里。
  • Windows 上打开添加或编辑供应商页面时窗口闪一下。 v4.0.9 修了编辑页先显示加载页的问题，但整页的淡入淡出在 Windows 上仍会闪。现在这两个页面不再做淡入淡出，其他页面不受影响。
  • Windows 上删掉的桌面快捷方式，每次更新都会被装回来。 MSI 升级会先卸载旧版再完整安装新版，以前每次都会重新创建桌面快捷方式。现在只在全新安装时创建；升级时只有原来的快捷方式还在才保留。

其他： 「连接模式」的说明改为先讲每种模式的长处：直连最原生，路由可热切换供应商、支持故障转移和格式转换，聚合可在客户端内无缝切换多家模型。用量页的所有表格列统一左对齐，请求日志里供应商和模型两列让出一些宽度给数字列。日文界面把「高級オプション」改为「詳細オプション」。

升级说明

数据库结构没有变化，仍然是 20。

  • 已经在用路由模式的 Claude Code，升级后启动 CC Switch 时会把新的 haiku 别名写进 settings.json，新开的 Claude Code 会话会用上。
  • Codex 会话的迁移在 CC Switch 启动时自动进行，每个 Codex 配置目录只做一次，改动前的会话记录备份在原有的会话分组迁移备份目录。如果你手动恢复过 cc-switch-official 表并且仍在使用，这次迁移会跳过。
  • 新增预设只影响新添加的供应商。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（OpenRouter 余额为零时显示套餐已过期、Codex 中途换模型后用量记错模型）、@hulkbig（设置自动保存时带上未确认的目录）、@NestorRay（Windows 上打开供应商页面时的闪烁）的贡献。

感谢 @hao-lin-lwh、@desmonna、@mossonli、@enderzcx、@nbcoming、@liyangdeamo-svg、@linhay、@aofee 报告问题、提供复现信息和帮忙验证。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。
